# A052 / B11 stage 2: optimal recentring cannot exploit larger blocks

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Follow B11 STREAM_NOTES v2: replace the prior affine-pivot cost by the fully optimized conditional mismatch beta_A, then decide whether some proper block of each iterated point-defect composition satisfies U_A=H(S_A|S_Ac)+(2/ln2)beta_A<=(H/I)I_A. Entropy is in bits, f is an actual sign on a finite uniform cube, S has law fhat^2, and I_A=sum_(i in A)P(i in S). Only nonempty proper blocks with I_A>0 are admitted. Constants never enter ratios.

The frozen finite inputs are A051's 1,150 saved read-once NAND shapes on 6..9 distinct literal leaves and seven explicitly defined Hod-named six/eight-bit seeds and fresh NAND hybrids. These are a precise finite formula list, not all Boolean functions or the large recursive lower-bound family. We do not repeat A051's decrement-minimum census. The new analytic theorem proves that the optimized upper certificate's block minimum equals its singleton minimum for every binary law. Its finite decisions therefore cover all proper blocks without a new block-orbit search.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read STATUS.md and LEARNED.md, the updated BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-12 00:39 UTC, and B11 STREAM_NOTES.md v2, 2026-10-11 23:3xZ. The board's B06 block/singleton comparison and B17 zero-erasure transport are relevant background; no uninspected board line is used as a proof. Read the lead's run 20261011T230100Z REPORT and CLAIMS, especially R1/R2 optimized recentring, R5 block reduction and R6 tensor scope. Reviewed the analytic interfaces independently in DEPENDENCY_REVIEW.md before adding this new candidate. R3/R4's finite scans are not proof dependencies; the source's floating computations do not provide an explicit checked global rounding bound for the whole scan.

Read A051's stage 20261011T233123Z REPORT, UNIVERSAL_DESCENT_SQUEEZE.md, COMPOSITION_CERTIFICATES.md, formula/seed definitions in descent_compositions.py and check_seeded_and_replay.py, and exact source summaries full_SUMMARY.json, SEEDED_FULL_SUMMARY.json and AGGREGATE_CERTIFICATES.json. The squeeze is independently reconstructed locally. It corrects the lead's phi/R<=1.33 experiment: revealing all but a least-influential bit already proves phi/R<=n/(n-1). This stage tests the different upper certificate and does not reinterpret that automatic ratio ceiling as payment.

The protocol remains EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, read in full in the previous stage and used again for the exact schemas. The prior A052 REPORT and CRITICAL_SOURCE_STRUCTURE.tex supply the self-contained logarithmic Sobolev proof and the affine comparison being sharpened. No external literature theorem, A080 asymptotic certificate, or percolation result is a new premise. Source hashes and locators are in out/SOURCE_MANIFEST.json, and both consumed summary hashes were frozen before computation.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

SINGLETON_COLLAPSE.md and CRITICAL_SOURCE_STRUCTURE.tex give the complete proof. For every binary law define B_A=H(S_A|S_Ac), beta_A=sum_(i in A)E min(P(i in S|S_Ac),P(i notin S|S_Ac)), and U_A=B_A+kappa beta_A, kappa=2/ln2. The conditional chain rule gives B_A>=sum_A B_i. Conditional Jensen for the concave function min(q,1-q), using conditioning on all other coordinates, gives beta_A>=sum_A beta_i. Thus U_A>=sum_A U_i, and normalization by I_A yields

    min_(nonempty proper A, I_A>0) U_A/I_A
       = min_(i:I_i>0) [B_i+kappa beta_i]/I_i.              (1)

Every relevant singleton is proper when n>=2. No Booleanity, extremality or optimizer existence is used in (1). For Boolean physical children, the independently reconstructed lead lemma gives J_A<=kappa beta_A. The criticality comparison enters only at L_A>=R I_A, or L_A>=KI_A+D_K for a dimension-critical K-witness. This restates the prior pivot obstruction with the optimal predictor. A larger block cannot rescue the U certificate if all singletons fail. The information-cap repair B_A+min(|A|,kappa beta_A) also collapses to singletons, by min(sum a_i,sum b_i)>=sum min(a_i,b_i).

There is an exact all-dimensional mechanism obstruction. For F_m=product_j (-1)^(x_j y_j), m>=2, the squared spectrum is uniform, so H=2m and I=m. Every block has B_A=|A|, beta_A=|A|/2, and I_A=|A|/2. Consequently U_A/I_A=2+kappa>R=2, and the capped version equals 4. Yet the actual decrement is L_A=2 times the number of touched pairs, and J_A is the number of pairs touched once. Revealing a whole pair has J_A=0 and L_A/I_A=2. All physical restrictions have ratio<=2, so this is genuinely non-strict physical-descendant ratio-critical; it is neither tensor-indecomposable nor dimension-minimal. The same certificate failure with strict actual descent holds for disjoint tensor powers of a two-bit quadratic factor times a three-bit point defect: the root ratio is between 2 and 3, each singleton certificate is at least 2+kappa>4, and querying the low-ratio full factor improves the remaining child. These are bounded-ratio route obstructions, not FEI disproofs or high-threshold critical obstructions.

Exact decisions on the saved NAND family are:

| dimension | formulas | U certificate passes | fails every block | failures with independently rechecked strict actual descent |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 6 | 33 | 8 | 25 | 8 |
| 7 | 90 | 20 | 70 | 37 |
| 8 | 261 | 60 | 201 | 102 |
| 9 | 766 | 177 | 589 | 332 |
| total | 1150 | 265 | 885 | 479 |

All seven separate seed/hybrid controls fail U_A<=R I_A; four nevertheless have directly rechecked strict actual descent. The latter is a constructive witness statement, independent of whether A051's chosen blocks are global decrement minimizers. We verify those blocks directly from truth tables. For NAND case 0006, a six-bit formula, R is between 2.40 and 2.41, min U_i/I_i exceeds 3.57, yet block mask 48 has L_A/I_A<2.30. Its actual joint channel and exact symbolic costs are in out/CHANNEL_REPRODUCTION.json. For Hod_g3, the certified displayed values are R approximately 2.414979, minimum U_i/I_i approximately 2.688444, and the actual singleton decrement approximately 2.348682. Displays summarize rational endpoint certificates, not floating signs.

First unsupported line: infer a fixed absolute K paying B_A+J_A on arbitrary dimension-critical witnesses, or the full terminal-inclusive stopping-tree cost. The finite census does not do this. Even a universal bound for the optimized U minimum would be a singleton erasure route; optimizing larger blocks in this same surrogate cannot create an additional payment. The all-dimensional counterexamples reject the own-ratio certificate and its elementary information-cap repair, not every larger fixed universal K or every joint-channel/critical-only mechanism.

For each actual truth table, integer Walsh squares sum exactly to N^2. Spectrum influence agrees with counted physical boundary edges; root entropy vectors and truth-table hashes agree exactly with the frozen sources. Natural-unit entropy has numerator N^2 ln(N^2)-sum w_s^2 ln(w_s^2), represented by integer prime-log coefficients. Marginal packet sums compute B_i; beta_i is the exact sum of packet minima. The tested sign is

    I_i_num H_num - I_num B_i_num - 2 beta_i_num I_num.

All coefficients and constants are integers. Its sign is checked with rational logarithm intervals. Range reduction uses ln p=k ln2+ln(p/2^k); the positive atanh series has 40 terms and a proved tail at most 2z^81/[81(1-z^2)], z<=1/3. Each log interval is rounded outward to a 96-bit dyadic interval. Signed expressions reverse endpoints for negative coefficients, and signed ratios use all four division corners. No floating sign decision is made. Equal minima are recognized by exact symbolic coefficients and constants. The precision is fixed, not silently escalated.

Every singleton's actual physical-child entropy is independently reconstructed; the channel upper bound is checked against that value. One named proper block from A051's summary is directly recomputed for every case, so all 479 positive actual-descent statements are new physical witnesses. The pilot additionally checks every proper block of its two NAND cases and both six-bit seeds for entropy/mismatch superadditivity and the actual channel bound. The separate replay uses direct character sums for roots and children, compares them with FWHT, and exhausts all proper blocks of the two quadratic tensors and the five-bit strict tensor example. It shares the proved rational-log enclosure and is not represented as an independent logarithm implementation or external review. Per-case certificates are saved immediately in out/cases; summaries and the channel replay are also saved. All files are below 20 MB.

NEXT targets the exact cancellation visible in a saved strict-descent block because it changes the failed additive mechanism at its first unsupported line, whereas a larger isolated formula census or a larger-block search using the same U bound cannot advance full FEI.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

HIDDEN_TENSOR_DIAGNOSTIC.md sections 1--7 contains the complete proof; NEAR_TENSOR_PERTURBATIONS.tex is the requested concise mathematical interface. With p=2^-n and g=f-2f(a)1_a, the exact influence change is 2p sum_i f(a)f(a^i). Additive mixed derivative energy changes by p(r_i+r_j-r_ij), while multiplicative plaquette probability changes by 4p sigma_ij(a). Across a product cut the latter is exactly 4p and centered spectral covariance has absolute value <=7p+4p^2. Raw additive mixed-derivative positivity is not a nonfactorization statistic.

Vanishing cross-cut plaquettes are equivalent to coordinate tensor factorization. A one-point flip of a nontrivial product changes the whole-cube product from +1 to -1, ruling out every nontrivial coordinate factorization. All integer Walsh sums are then 2 mod4, proving full support. These are exact conditions, not visual circuit connectedness.

Parseval and Cauchy-Schwarz bound spectral TV by 2sqrt(p(1-p)). A self-contained maximal-coupling proof gives the entropy continuity bound. For n>=4, |H(g)-H(f)|<=h2(u_n)+n u_n, u_n=2^(1-floor(n/2)); influence error is <=2np. Both estimates are uniform in tensor count, core dimensions and point location and include zero old coefficients.

For any fixed nonconstant d-bit core h, balanced powers F_t=z*h^tensor t have H=tH(h), I=tI(h)+1. Their one-point surgeries G_t are tensor-prime, full-support, all-essential and have connected plaquette graphs, yet H(G_t)/I(G_t) tends to H(h)/I(h). Across the fresh-sign cut the total plaquette curvature is 4td*2^(-td-1), tending to zero, and the corresponding summed absolute covariance also tends to zero. The simultaneous qualitative class therefore has the same extended optimal FEI constant as unrestricted Boolean signs. This is an admission equivalence, not an actual unbounded-ratio family.

For t>=2 fixing another copy away from its surgery coordinates removes the defect and leaves +/-h as a proper physical descendant. Thus a strict violating core makes these admitted embeddings fail dimension-criticality. A051's stronger all-descendant tests remain distinct and useful.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

CONTINUITY_PROOF.md contains the complete analytic derivation, with NEAR_TENSOR_DESCENT.tex as a concise mathematical interface. A one-point flip affects exactly one of a block's 2^a children, giving |delta L_A|<=E_n+2^-a E_(n-a); the physical influence change obeys |delta I_A|<=2a2^-n. The E_m envelope includes dimensions zero and one, where Boolean spectral entropy is always zero, and zero old coefficients. A second continuity bound uses the full actual-query/retained-spectrum channel and common-mixture conditional entropy, with no dimension dependence on the retained spectral alphabet.

If every seed coordinate has influence at least kappa>2^(1-n), the proper-block family is unchanged and the explicit two-sided error is

    |delta phi|<=max_(1<=a<n) [E_n+2^-a E_(n-a)]/[a(kappa-2p)]
                         +4p/[kappa(kappa-2p)], p=2^-n.

The same argument bounds the H/I error. A strict seed slack larger than the sum of these errors transfers phi(surgery)<H(surgery)/I(surgery). A selected cut with I_A>2ap has a one-sided transfer without requiring healthy influence on other coordinates. Denominator assumptions and fixed physical-coordinate conventions are explicit; surgery can otherwise change which blocks are admissible.

The lead's suggested exact inheritance at equality is false. For every n>=2 the tensor parity F_n=chi_[n] has phi=H/I=0, but G_n=chi_[n](1-2*1_0), its actual one-point surgery, has phi(G_n)>H(G_n)/I(G_n). The exact residual at every proper block A is 2(n-|A|)2^-n(R_n-R_(n-|A|))>0, where R_n is the strictly increasing point-defect H/I ratio. Monotonicity is proved analytically by R_n=4-B_n/n, with B_n positive and decreasing; it is not inferred from a finite list. Both phi(G_n) and H/I are asymptotic to 8*2^-n, and 1<phi/(H/I)<=n/(n-1). Continuity holds while the exact inequality stays reversed.

CLAUDE-B11-R6@1 on exact products remains valid. LEAD_SCOPE_CORRECTION.md pins the unversioned inference that needs narrowing, independently reconstructs R6's factor-ratio clause, and distinguishes the correction from refuting R6. This actual Boolean family has defect mass tending to zero and uniformly healthy seed influences; the issue is zero slack, not a denominator pathology. It has bounded vanishing H/I, so neither a fixed positive universal descent constant nor FEI is refuted.

For n>=3 all averaged proper-block tests at C=H(G_n)/I(G_n) pass strictly, yet an edited two-bit physical child has ratio 2 greater than the root ratio. Thus averaging cannot replace the individual child tests of genuine criticality. Entropy-positive fixed cores with a fresh sign have phi=0 and a positive limiting root ratio, so the strict-slack theorem does give eventual exact descent for their surgeries. This retains the original arbitrary-core admission result and A055's criticality exclusion.

First unsupported universal line: every genuine high-threshold dimension-critical source has a quantitative factor-cut slack or another affordable proper block at one fixed finite absolute price, with full individual child/terminal deficits retained. No bound on sup_n K_desc(n), dimension-independent critical-source separation, or universal FEI constant is proved.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

CRITICAL_INTERFACE_PAYMENT.md sections 1--5 contains the complete proof;
COMPONENT_EXCESS.tex supplies the concise theorem interface. The exact
raw block drop is B_A+(1/ln2)sum_t Ent(v_t²), where B_A is the mass-weighted
conditional selector spectral entropy. Unlike Boolean signs, indicators
have child-mass tilt, and their normalized channel includes
D(P_X||Uniform) in addition to mutual information. This term is retained.
Cube logarithmic Sobolev pays the squared-amplitude entropy by
(2/ln2)E_A, with a self-contained two-point proof and tensorization.

When each physical selector fiber meets at most one actual component,
the child raw entropies sum exactly. This gives the component comparison
with Gamma=[B_A+kappa E_A-sum_k Lambda_A(g_k)]_+, where the component
credits are explicit partial-Fourier quantities. No entropy gap defines
Gamma. For selector subcube cylinders, Lambda_A(g_k)=2d_k p_k=4E_A(g_k).
If d_k>=|A|/2 then E_A>=|A|p/4 and Gamma<=kappa E_A. Energy additivity
gives a corresponding ratio-max bound WITH its interface retained.

The exact criticality line is Rraw E_A<=Lambda_A; it gives
Rraw<=4+2/ln2 on the thin-cylinder critical class. For actual sign
criticality, R I_A<=H(f)-average H(children)<=|A|+kappa I_A and
I_A>=|A|p. Hence R<=kappa+1/p0 when p>=p0; in particular
H<=(4+2/ln2)I on the stated critical class with 1/4<=p<=1/2.
Threshold-critical witnesses at K>=kappa+1/p0 are likewise excluded.

First unsupported universal line: every prospective dimension-minimal
violating sign admits this separator geometry with uniformly positive
support density, or another affordable proper-block descent. Rare
support, non-subcube components and unrestricted terminal cores remain
unpaid. The geometric class can admit arbitrary data cores, but their
criticality is not inherited or asserted. Full FEI remains OPEN.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROJECTION_PAYMENT.md sections1--5 is the complete proof, and
COMPONENT_EXCESS.tex is the concise interface. For any physical block,
raw component excess is exactly the signed block cross term
Lambda_A(g)-sum_k Lambda_A(g_k), PLUS the mean labelled child excess.
Only a component-isolating block, equivalently disjoint physical
component projections, makes that terminal term vanish. Component
labels are preserved if children further disconnect.

For a real partial-Fourier row supported on a fraction mu of the
physical queried cube, Cauchy--Schwarz bounds every normalized Fourier
atom by mu. Its Fourier entropy is therefore at least log2(1/mu).
The physical squared-row density has KL to uniform at least the same
quantity. Consequently a component of mass p_k and projection measure
mu_k has Lambda_A(g_k)>=2p_k log2(1/mu_k), without product structure.
The raw mass-tilt term is retained throughout. Fiberwise indicator edge
isoperimetry, proved by induction with the exact energy normalization,
also gives E_A(g_k)>=p_k log2(1/mu_k)/2.

Write Q_A=sum_k p_k log2(1/mu_k), p=sum_kp_k, r=|A|, and
Theta_A=[rp-2Q_A]_+. The independently defined interface satisfies
Gamma_A<=kappa E_A+Theta_A, kappa=2/ln2. If Q_A>=rp/2, then
Gamma_A<=kappa E_A and E_A>=rp/4. This removes the stage1 Cartesian,
subcube and constant-fiber assumptions and also replaces individual
selector-width restrictions by a weighted projection condition.
On this stated critical class Rraw<=4+kappa. For actual sign physical
criticality with p>=p0, H/I<=kappa+1/p0; at p0=1/4 this is 4+kappa.
The root and proper-child deficits are retained for fixed-K minimal
violators, excluding the class at K>=kappa+1/p0. Universal admission
to the projection/density class is not assumed.

The seven-bit Brain45 sign has root H/I in
(2.321169,2.321170) and phi in (2.302128,2.302129), uniquely minimized
by mask15, querying all four data coordinates. Exactly,

    phi_sign = 188/25 - (369/800)log2 3 - (1587/1600)log2 23.

The root has influence 103/64; this queried block consumes 25/16 and
leaves mean child influence 3/64. Its sole nonconstant child is the
three-control-bit point defect with ratio
16/3-(3/2)log2 3, strictly above the root ratio. Thus the root fails
physical ratio criticality at an explicit child, and the terminal
positive root-ratio deficit is retained rather than discarded.
Raw phi is in (3.730780,3.730781), minimized by the six masks
3,5,6,9,10,12, each querying two of the four data bits. These are exact
minimizers over all126 proper nonempty blocks, with symbolic ties and
rigorous comparisons; the control is not an own-ratio sign descent
obstruction.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

RAW_SIGN_TRANSFER.md sections1--5 is the full proof; COMPONENT_EXCESS.tex
is the concise interface. Exact Fourier accounting shows that natural
sign entropy equals four times natural raw entropy plus
T(p)=-(1-2p)^2ln((1-2p)^2)+4p^2ln(p^2)-8ln2 p(1-p).
The block correction is exactly T(p)-average T(p_x), retaining every
actual child mass. Its second derivative simplifies to
16ln(2p/abs(1-2p)). The bound pT''>=-8/e proves convexity of
T+(8/e)p ln p; Jensen controls the correction by (8/e)Ent_x(p_x).
Logarithmic Sobolev on sqrt(p_x), followed by the L2 reverse triangle
inequality for actual indicator children, pays Ent_x(p_x)<=2E_A(g).
No lower density bound, non-Boolean surrogate or assumed criticality
enters this derivation.

Consequently L_A(f)<=4Lambda_A(g)+(4/(e ln2))I_A(f) for every physical
block. The same-leaf-partition finite-tree telescope retains actual
leaf H and F and charges only erased influence. It transfers a raw
tree rate K to a sign tree rate K+4/(e ln2), without a repeated root
bank. Zero queried influence makes the correction exactly zero.

The prior component projection credits imply E_A>=rp/4 and
Lambda_A<=(4+2/ln2)E_A whenever a proper component-isolating block has
Q_A=sum_k p_k log2(1/mu_k)>=rp/2. Therefore its actual sign decrement
is paid at C_proj=4+2/ln2+4/(e ln2), certified in
(9.008341, 9.008342) and analytically below37/4<10.
Physical sign ratio criticality now gives H/I<=C_proj at EVERY positive
support density, including rare non-Cartesian support. This removes
the preceding p>=p0 hypothesis, though the older moderately balanced
bound remains sharper within its narrower scope. A minimal fixed-K
violator obeys D_K+S_A<= (C_proj-K)I_A, with complete actual-child
reserve S_A>=0. K>=C_proj excludes the stated projection class.

overlapping component projections. Rarity is no longer the missing
assumption for this class. Full FEI remains OPEN; the transfer theorem
does not assert global raw payment or universal proper-block admission.

# Universal bridge

This stage advances C019 stopping-tree payment and B11 critical block
selection by a universal raw-indicator-to-sign correction price of
4/(e ln2) times erased influence on the same leaf partition. It removes
the positive-density assumption from the component-projection critical
class at C_proj<37/4 while retaining all child reserves, with
32491 exact checks. What remains is universal proper
geometric admission or signed payment of projection overlap and labelled
terminal cross terms; rarity no longer blocks this transfer. NEXT attacks
that remaining physical-child selection line because a uniform paid

SOURCE ~\brain_workers\FEI100_20261011\agents\A056\runs\20261011T214407Z-fleet05-A056-balanced-composition\REPORT.md
# A056: balanced disjoint substitution and its physical restrictions

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite k and pairwise disjoint physical coordinate blocks B_i, let g_i:{-1,1}^{B_i}->{-1,1}, let h:{-1,1}^k->{-1,1}, and put F=h(g_1,...,g_k). Physical coordinates are independent uniform signs. Entropy H is Shannon entropy in bits of the **entire** squared Fourier spectrum, including the empty coefficient; I is expected Fourier-set size, equivalently total flip influence. No restriction on the mean of h is needed. First assume every g_i is balanced: E g_i=0. Then

    E F = E h,
    Var F = Var h,
    H(F) = H(h) + sum_i Inf_i(h) H(g_i),
    I(F) = sum_i Inf_i(h) I(g_i),
    Inf_x(F) = Inf_i(h) Inf_x(g_i),  x in B_i.

This packet proves these identities, extends them to arbitrary child means, and identifies precisely what must be updated after a physical restriction. These are composition baseline identities, not a proof of unrestricted FEI. The complete analytic proof is local; independent acceptance is pending.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### Balanced coefficient identity

Write g_i=sum_{U subset B_i} c_i(U) chi_U and h=sum_{T subset [k]} a(T) chi_T. Balance means c_i(empty)=0. For a physical Fourier set S, put S_i=S intersect B_i and T(S)={i:S_i is nonempty}. Expanding h after substitution, a term indexed by T can contribute to S only if T=T(S): unused blocks contribute the constant 1, and every used block has a nonempty set. Disjoint blocks make the union unique. Hence, including S=empty,

    Fhat(S) = a(T(S)) product_{i in T(S)} c_i(S_i).                  (1)

Parseval gives sum_{U nonempty} c_i(U)^2=1. Thus the spectral law is exactly the following experiment: sample T with probability a(T)^2; independently for each i in T sample a nonempty U_i with probability c_i(U_i)^2; output S=union U_i. Conversely S recovers T and every U_i. The experiment is bijective on positive-probability outcomes, so its entropy is H(h)+sum_i P(i in T)H(g_i). Since P(i in T)=Inf_i(h), this proves the entropy formula, with no latent-label correction or omitted empty atom. Its expected size is sum_i Inf_i(h)I(g_i). The probability a given x in B_i is included is Inf_i(h)Inf_x(g_i). Finally (1) at empty proves E F=E h and therefore the variance formula. Zero coefficients are simply omitted; 0 log(1/0)=0.

### Arbitrary means: reexpanded outer gate

Let m_i=E g_i and v_i=1-m_i^2. First absorb all deterministic children (v_i=0) into h by fixing those output coordinates to m_i. This is exact because a Boolean uniform-cube function of mean +/-1 is constant at every input. Reindex the remaining children; now v_i>0. Their independent output law is mu, with P(Y_i=+1)=(1+m_i)/2. In its orthonormal basis psi_i(y)=(y_i-m_i)/sqrt(v_i), write

    h(y)=sum_T a_mu(T) product_{i in T} psi_i(y).

Define P_mu(T)=a_mu(T)^2, w_i=sum_{T containing i} P_mu(T), H_mu(h)=H(P_mu), and I_mu(h)=sum_T |T|P_mu(T). These biased spectral influences w_i are not the ordinary unweighted pivotal probabilities. Indeed w_i=v_i E_mu[(D_i h)^2], where D_i h=(h(y_i=+1)-h(y_i=-1))/2 on the other outputs.

On the physical block,

    (g_i-m_i)/sqrt(v_i)=sum_{U nonempty} c_i(U)/sqrt(v_i) chi_U.

This again has zero empty coefficient and squared mass one. The same unique union argument gives

    Fhat(S)=a_mu(T(S)) product_{i in T(S)} c_i(S_i)/sqrt(v_i).       (2)

The child's nonempty spectral law is q_i(U)=c_i(U)^2/v_i. Its entropy K_i satisfies

    H(g_i)=h2(v_i)+v_i K_i,                                        (3)

because the empty atom has mass m_i^2=1-v_i. The exact full identities are consequently

    E F = E_mu h = a_mu(empty),       Var F=Var_mu h,
    H(F)=H_mu(h)+sum_i (w_i/v_i)[H(g_i)-h2(v_i)],                   (4)
    I(F)=sum_i (w_i/v_i) I(g_i),
    Inf_x(F)=(w_i/v_i)Inf_x(g_i), x in B_i.                        (5)

Equations (2)-(5) are valid at every finite dimension, for arbitrary nonconstant child means. All entropy terms are finite; no limiting assertion is needed. If every child was deterministic, F is constant and all entropies and influences are zero. Outer irrelevant coordinates have w_i=0 and require no child contribution. Balanced children give v_i=1, h2(v_i)=0 and the uniform outer law, recovering the first formulas.

For comparison with Proposition 7.1, the strong-entropy convention must be distinguished from conditional nonempty entropy. The convention that actually composes is Eplus(g)=sum_{U nonempty} c(U)^2 log2(1/c(U)^2) on uniform physical inputs, and Eplus_mu(h)=sum_{T nonempty} P_mu(T)log2(product v_i/P_mu(T)) on outputs. Then exactly

    Eplus(F)=Eplus_mu(h)+sum_i (w_i/v_i) Eplus(g_i).                (6)

It follows immediately from (2), with no Var(F) numerator. The conditional nonempty entropy in (3), namely H(g)-h2(Var(g)), is a different quantity. This run's main identities (1)-(5) deliberately use full H or conditional K, so do not depend on a strong-entropy naming convention. Define deficit delta=I-Var. Since sum_i w_i=I_mu(h), and Var(F)=Var_mu(h),

    delta(F)=delta_mu(h)+sum_i (w_i/v_i)delta(g_i).                 (7)

### Physical restrictions: what survives, and what changes

Fix actual physical coordinates W to a deterministic assignment rho. In each block replace g_i by g_i^rho=g_i restricted to W intersect B_i. Remaining inputs retain the independent uniform law and disjoint supports. Therefore (2)-(5) hold with the **new** means m_i^rho, variances v_i^rho, absorbed deterministic children, and the Fourier expansion of the reduced outer gate under the **new** output product law. They do not generally hold with old a_mu or old w_i. The balanced formulas are available at a reached branch when every remaining nonconstant child is balanced after absorption of constants. If only one child is restricted, all untouched initially balanced children remain balanced, but the changed child must be checked. Balance of that child is sufficient; it is not claimed logically necessary for accidental numerical equality in a special gate.

For a single initially balanced child j, write the uniform outer polynomial as h=A(y_-j)+y_j B(y_-j). Let a_T=Ahat(T), b_T=Bhat(T), and let the changed child's mean be m, variance v=1-m^2>0. Other children stay balanced. Recenter:

    h=A+mB+sqrt(v) psi_j B.

The new outer coefficients are a_T+m b_T on T not containing j, and sqrt(v)b_T on T union {j}. Thus squared weights include the coherent term 2m a_T b_T; they cannot be replaced by separate a_T^2 and m^2 b_T^2. Direct physical coefficients are (a_T+m b_T) product_{i in T} c_i(S_i) when S_j is empty, and b_T c_j^rho(S_j) product_{i in T} c_i(S_i) otherwise. The j-block physical multiplier is still sum_T b_T^2=Inf_j(h), because w_j^rho=v sum_T b_T^2. Other block multipliers generally change.

If v=0, fix outer y_j=m, giving the actual reduced polynomial A+mB. Do not divide by v. If m=0, the original outer coefficients remain valid. If the restriction is chosen adaptively using already observed physical values, each fixed leaf of the physical decision tree is a cylinder and its unrevealed coordinates are independent uniform. The formulas apply branchwise. An expectation over leaves must retain the branch-specific outer laws and weights; it cannot silently substitute the initial gate's spectrum. Conditioning only on a child output instead of fixing physical coordinates is not such a cylinder and need not leave a product physical law.

### Exact witness against retaining the old balanced formulas

Let h(y,z)=AND_2(y,z) in sign notation: +1 only when both inputs are +1. Its uniform spectrum has four coefficients of magnitude 1/2, so E h=-1/2, H(h)=2, and Inf_y(h)=Inf_z(h)=1/2. Let the first initially balanced child be majority on three signs (x,a,b), and the second be a fresh dictator z. Physically fixing x=+1 changes the first child to OR_2(a,b), with mean 1/2, variance 3/4, H=2 and I=1. The restricted F=AND_2(OR_2(a,b),z) has constant coefficient -1/4, z coefficient 3/4, and the other six coefficients of magnitude 1/4. Its spectrum is seven atoms 1/16 and one atom 9/16. Hence

    E F=-1/4,     I(F)=5/4,
    H(F)=4-(9/16)log2(9)=4-(9/8)log2(3).

Retaining old balanced formulas predicts E F=-1/2, I(F)=1, H(F)=3. All three predictions are false. For the entropy comparison, 3 > H(F) follows exactly from 3^9>2^8, with no floating point. The new output-biased gate weights are 1/16, 3/16, 9/16, 3/16 on empty, {y}, {z}, {y,z}. Thus w_y=3/8 and w_z=3/4, and (5) gives (3/8)/(3/4)+(3/4)=5/4. This is an actual Boolean physical restriction of an initially balanced substitution, not an arbitrary spectral law.

### Scope of consequences

Ordinary bounds H(h)<=C I(h) and H(g_i)<=C I(g_i) alone do not give the same C under the balanced identity: the outer H(h) remains an additional term. A sufficient balanced compositional invariant is H(q)<=C[I(q)-Var(q)] for the gate and all children. Since Var(g_i)=1, the formulas give H(F)<=C[I(F)-Var(F)]. This is a conditional consequence of explicitly stated gate/child bounds; those bounds are not proved for arbitrary Boolean functions here. After a physical restriction, either verify balance again or use product-biased identities and gate hypotheses at the new means. An arbitrary weighted threshold theorem established only for uniform inputs cannot be applied at newly biased child outputs. No overlap theorem, selector credit, or full FEI conclusion follows from these identities alone.

There is no unsupported line in the proofs of (1)-(5), (6)-(7), or the witness. General dimension-independent FEI remains open. The unpaid next research term is a quantitative entropy/influence payment for changed biased gate laws or overlapping-block coherent coefficients; it is not supplied by disjoint identities.

Entropy identities are checked exactly as rational linear combinations of logarithms of primes; equality of such vectors is a sufficient exact equality certificate, and no numerical logarithms are used. This is finite corroboration of the analytic proof, not an all-dimensional inference.

Pilot: 16 outer gates with two balanced two-bit children, all passed; measured computation time 0.011620600009337068 seconds. Main command: python -X utf8 code/check.py from this run directory. Its output is out/checks.json. Planned exhaustive domain: all 16 outer gates and all 16^2 two-bit-child pairs (4096 cases, including 576 balanced cases), all 16 gates and all six balanced two-bit children under all nine partial restrictions paired with a dictator (864 cases), and the explicit majority/AND witness. No symmetry reduction, random sampling, dimension escalation or heavy job. See checks.json for the actually completed count and runtime.

SOURCE ~\brain_workers\FEI100_20261011\agents\A056\runs\20261011T233147Z-fleet05-A056-balanced-composition\REPORT.md
# A056 stage 2: biased-child erasure and physical entropy decrement

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Extend the adopted A056 composition interface, as directed by B12 STREAM_NOTES v2 (23:30 UTC), to every physical query inside a potentially biased child. Let F=h(g_1,...,g_k), with Boolean sign children on mutually disjoint independent uniform physical blocks. Delete deterministic other children first. Fix child j and a physical coordinate i inside it; the selected child may be biased. Entropies here are **bits**, whereas A057 and the lead's L1 formulas use nats. Write B_i(f)=H(1_{i in S}|S without i) for the squared Fourier law and D_i(f)=H(f)-[H(f_i^+)+H(f_i^-)]/2. D_i denotes entropy decrement, not spectral deficit or the character-centered influence.

This stage supplies exact B_i and D_i splits, their signed empty-pair corrections, and a positive-sector bound valid below the root after actual physical restrictions. It does not establish a universal stopping-tree payment. This is a focused completion of the recentered interface, not a new broad gate-family candidate; B12's review-cap direction is retained.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-11 23:29 UTC, B12 STREAM_NOTES.md v2 23:30 UTC, and own STATUS.md at stage start. The lead adopts A056's first-stage disjoint identities and directs this biased-child extension. A091 REPORT.md independently accepts A056 v1 at scope by a separate coefficient reconstruction; this review is consumed, not merely inferred from the board. A057 REPORT.md supplies the balanced B/JS split, the paid explicit logarithm, its precise unsupported universal line, and NEXT_HANDOFF's proposed signed biased correction. A057's claims are still pending review; the present derivation independently reconstructs all identities used. EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json v2 was reread; its required schemas and review cap apply. Local byte hashes of these sources and prior A056 proof/interface are in out/source_manifest.json. Earlier general campaign and paper source pins remain those in the stage-1 manifest; no new whole-manuscript audit or external lookup is claimed.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Pair functionals and the exact coordinate identity

Define phi(s)=s log2(1/s) for s>0, phi(0)=0, and

    e(s,t)=phi(s)+phi(t)-phi(s+t),
    d(a,b)=phi(a^2)+phi(b^2)-[phi((a+b)^2)+phi((a-b)^2)]/2,
    j(a,b)=d(a,b)-e(a^2,b^2).

All three relevant functions are homogeneous of degree one in squared amplitudes: e(cs,ct)=c e(s,t), and d(sqrt(c)a,sqrt(c)b)=c d(a,b), likewise j. In particular e(s,t)=(s+t)h2(t/(s+t)), and

    j(a,b)=(a^2+b^2)[1-h2(1/2+ab/(a^2+b^2))] >= 0.             (1)

For a physical Fourier pair a_U=fhat(U), b_U=fhat(U union {i}), where U excludes i,

    B_i(f)=sum_U e(a_U^2,b_U^2),
    D_i(f)=sum_U d(a_U,b_U),
    JS_i(f)=D_i(f)-B_i(f)=sum_U j(a_U,b_U).                       (2)

Indeed restriction replaces the pair by a_U+b_U or a_U-b_U. The average pair mass equals a_U^2+b_U^2, so subtracting the raw entropies proves (2), without normalization or omitted empty pair. Formulas (1)-(2) apply even if a pair has zero mass.

### 2. Recentered outer coefficients and exact biased splits

Write h(y)=A(y_-j)+y_j B(y_-j). Expand A and B in the **normalized product basis of the other child outputs under their current means**, not necessarily under the uniform output law. Let their coefficients be a_T,b_T. For the chosen child, put

    m=E g_j, v=1-m^2, beta=g_jhat({i}), t=beta^2,
    C_T=a_T+m b_T, q=sum_T b_T^2=E[B(Y_-j)^2].

Since h is Boolean, B is 0 or +/-1 and q is its pivotal probability under the other outputs. For v>0 the recentered outer coefficients are C_T and sqrt(v)b_T; its biased spectral j-inclusion is w_j=vq. The physical multiplier is q, including when other children are biased. The actual influence identity is

    Inf_i(F)=q Inf_i(g_j).                                      (3)

Define the positive outer functions

    M(t)=sum_T e(C_T^2,t b_T^2),
    N(t)=sum_T j(C_T,beta b_T).

N depends only on beta^2, since j is even in either amplitude. Define the child's residual sectors, with precisely its empty i-deleted pair removed,

    B_i^*(g_j)=B_i(g_j)-e(m^2,t),
    D_i^*(g_j)=D_i(g_j)-d(m,beta).

Both are nonnegative, as sums of the nonempty U sectors in (2). Then the exact identities are

    B_i(F)=q B_i^*(g_j)+M(t),                                   (4)
    D_i(F)=q D_i^*(g_j)+M(t)+N(t).                              (5)

Equivalently, define signed corrections

    L_m(t)=M(t)-q e(m^2,t),
    K_m(t)=N(t)-q j(m,beta).

Then

    B_i(F)=q B_i(g_j)+L_m(t),
    D_i(F)=q D_i(g_j)+L_m(t)+K_m(t).                            (6)

Proof: for a nonempty selected-child Fourier set U, both coordinate-pair amplitudes have multiplier b_T, times the normalized nonempty spectra of occupied other child blocks. Homogeneity and Parseval on those child laws give q times the corresponding child pair contribution. For U=empty, the no-i amplitude is C_T, while the with-i amplitude is beta b_T. Parseval eliminates the other-block factors, giving M or M+N. The physical union identifies T and each other child's nonempty set, so no two distinct outer terms are squared incoherently. This also proves (3). Signed recentering is carried by C_T=a_T+m b_T throughout.

When m=0, e(m^2,t)=j(m,beta)=0, so (6) reduces to A057's balanced identity. When t=0, M=N=0 and the selected empty pair contributes nothing. When v=0, the child is constant, beta=0, its B/D and every query influence are zero; fix the gate coordinate and do not divide by v. Irrelevant gates have q=0 and all terms vanish. These statements cover every endpoint.

### 3. Positive-sector envelope valid at biased descendants

For v>0, Parseval gives 0<=t<=v. As a function of s>=0, M(s) is nonnegative, nondecreasing, concave, and M(0)=0. Each e(C^2,s b^2) has derivative b^2 log2(1+C^2/(s b^2)) when s b^2>0; its second derivative is nonpositive, with zero-amplitude cases handled by continuity. At s=v,

    M(v)=B_j^mu(h),                                            (7)

where B_j^mu means coordinate erasure of the **normalized biased outer squared spectrum**. This is a spectral conditional entropy, not the erasure under a uniform outer law and not a pivotal probability.

Set theta=t/v. Concavity of phi yields, term by term,

    M(t) <= theta M(v)+q t log2(v/t),
    M(t) <= M(v).

Thus, with t log(v/t)=0 when t=0,

    M(t) <= min{B_j^mu(h),
                 (t/v) B_j^mu(h)+q t log2(v/t)}.                (8)

To check the first inequality explicitly, phi(C^2+theta v b^2)>=(1-theta)phi(C^2)+theta phi(C^2+v b^2), while phi(theta v b^2)=theta phi(v b^2)+theta v b^2 log2(1/theta). Sum the resulting e inequality. No positivity of the signed L_m or K_m is assumed; the envelope applies to M.

The one-bit logarithmic Sobolev inequality gives

    0<=N(t)<= (2/ln 2) sum_T min(C_T^2,t b_T^2)
             <= (2/ln 2)q t.                                  (9)

Here is a self-contained scalar proof of the inequality used. For x>=y>=0, Ent_uniform((x^2,y^2)) in nats is at most (x-y)^2/2. By homogeneity take y=1. The difference F(x) between this upper bound and the entropy satisfies F(1)=F'(1)=F''(1)=0 and F'''(x)=2(x^2-1)/(x(x^2+1)^2)>=0; integrate three times for x>=1. The y=0 endpoint follows by continuity. Apply it to x=|a+b|, y=|a-b| (interchange if needed); their squared difference is 4 min(a^2,b^2). Division by ln2 proves j(a,b)<=2 min(a^2,b^2)/ln2. This proves (9), independently of an unreviewed imported estimate.

Combining (5),(8),(9) gives the exact positive-sector interface with the outer logarithmic remainder retained. In particular D_i(F) is bounded by q D_i^*(g_j) plus the right-hand side of (8) plus (2/ln2)qt.

### 4. What is paid once in a physical stopping tree

The Boolean derivative takes values 0,+/-1, so |beta|=|E D_i g_j|<=E[(D_i g_j)^2]=Inf_i(g_j). Since v<=1,

    q t log2(v/t) <= q t log2(1/t)
                   <= [2/(e ln2)] q |beta|
                   <= [2/(e ln2)] Inf_i(F).                    (10)

The scalar middle bound maximizes u ln(1/u) at u=1/e. Also qt<=Inf_i(F), so (9) costs at most (2/ln2)Inf_i(F). At every node of an adaptive **physical** restriction tree, the remaining blocks stay disjoint and the unrevealed physical signs stay independent uniform; recompute all child means and the current other-output basis. Therefore these two components have cumulative expected price at most

    [(2+2/e)/ln2] [I(root)-E I(leaf)].                          (11)

This uses the physical-query influence telescope once. It does not recharge a whole-subformula bank at each depth. Formula (11) pays only the explicit logarithm and outer JS component; it does not pay q D_i^*(child), the capped biased outer-erasure terms, or terminal entropy.

### 5. Restriction control and a signed-correction obstruction

For the required AND2(Maj3(x,a,b),z) control, first fix x=+1. The selected child is OR2(a,b) with m=1/2, v=3/4, beta=1/2 at either a or b. The other child is a fresh dictator. The recentered gate has C_empty=-1/4, C_{z}=3/4 and b_empty=b_{z}=1/2, so q=1/2. Equations (4)-(6) retain the child's nonzero empty pair e(1/4,1/4)=1/2 and d(1/2,1/2)=1. Its full B_i is 1 and D_i is 2, hence B_i^*=1/2 and D_i^*=1. Independently restricting a gives a dictator z at a=+1 (H=0), and AND2(b,z) at a=-1 (H=2). Thus

    D_a(F)=H(F)-1=3-(9/8)log2(3),
    Inf_a(F)=1/4,
    B_a(F)=1/4+M(1/4).

The exact coefficients and prime-log vectors are saved in out/checks.json. The total influence of this restricted F remains 5/4, as adopted in stage 1.

Signed corrections need not be nonnegative at biased descendants. Take h=AND2 and both children OR2 on disjoint two-bit blocks. For a selected coordinate of the first child, m=1/2, beta=1/2, q=3/4; the other child has mean 1/2 and variance 3/4. The signed erasure correction is

    L_m(1/4)= 1/4-(9/8)log2(3)+(5/8)log2(5)<0.                (12)

Indeed (12) is negative exactly when 4*5^5<3^9 (12500<19683), an integer comparison. The same fixture has K_m(1/4)=5/4-(5/8)log2(5)=(5/8)log2(4/5)<0, certified simply by 4<5. This directly prevents silently applying the balanced nonnegative-L/JS bank to biased children. The positive M,N envelope remains valid. The finite census records 512 negative L and 512 negative K cases; their exact signs use integer products, never decimals. These are scope controls inside the same interface extension, not additional broad candidate claims.

### 6. Honest first unsupported line

For a represented physical stopping tree, the unproved universal line is a dimension-independent bound on

    E H(leaf) + E sum_visited [q_v D_{i_v}^*(g_{v,j_v})
                 + min{B_{j_v}^{mu_v}(h_v),
                       (beta_v^2/v_v) B_{j_v}^{mu_v}(h_v)}]

by K I(root), with any legitimate allocation of the paid logarithmic terms and without repeatedly spending the same influence or dropping the negative empty-sector credit. Our estimates justify such a residual target but do not prove it. The displayed min is the smaller gate-only part after applying the two alternative bounds; a proof must couple that choice with the associated logarithmic bill. Using min of gate parts and paying the logarithm regardless is a valid upper bound. Arbitrary terminal child entropy remains present, so admission of an arbitrary hard core has not been bypassed.

## executed validation with domain, arithmetic, commands and outputs

out/FROZEN_SPEC.json was saved before search. code/check.py uses exact Fraction Fourier amplitudes, raw entropy vectors in rational prime-log coefficients, and denominator-cleared integer-product sign comparisons. It reconstructs D independently from actual truth-table restrictions as well as pair amplitudes. Domain: all 16 two-input gates and all 16^2 pairs of two-bit Boolean children on disjoint blocks, all four physical coordinates (16,384 splits), plus four named control splits. Constants are included as endpoint cases; no symmetry reduction or dimension escalation. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md gives a complete all-dimensional proof. Its reusable selector statement is: with sign controller a=1-2c, p=E c, Va=Var(a), branch means m,n, and d=(1-mn)/2, the actual controller path cost satisfies

    Pi_H=H(cu+(1-c)v)-pH(u)-(1-p)H(v)
         <=d Hplus(a)+C0 d Va,
    C0=2+2/ln2+1/(sqrt(e)ln2)<6.

The data branches can be arbitrarily biased and nonconstant. Their empty-row amplitudes collide coherently through M=p m+(1-p)n; the exact correction retains eta(M^2)-p eta(m^2)-(1-p)eta(n^2). The nonempty data rows are separated only by the physical disjointness assumption. The exact controller and terminal influence decomposition is d I(a)+p I(u)+(1-p)I(v).

This removes the apparent additional branch-bias surcharge: the same C0 as A028's constant-versus-core activation suffices for two distinct branches. The proof uses z=(m-n)^2/4<=d^2, Vu+Vv=4(d-z), semiconvexity of eta(mean^2), and an exact scalar maximization. The all-bias cases p=0,1, d=0 and z=0 require no division; their limiting formulas are direct.

For E1={s=+,A=1}, E2={s=+,B=1}, give E1 priority. With private probabilities p,q and negative core masses delta_u,delta_v, reveal s, determine A only on s=+, and determine B only on s=+,A=0. Stop at u,v, or +1. The exact four influence accounts are

    Q_s=p delta_u+(1-p)q delta_v,
    Q_A=(1/2)(delta_u+q delta_v-2delta_u q delta_v) I(1-2A),
    Q_B=(1/2)(1-p)delta_v I(1-2B),
    I_terminal=(1/2)p I(u)+(1/2)(1-p)q I(v).

The shared physical bit is charged exactly Q_s once, and the full root influence is the sum of these four accounts. Actual terminal entropy is (p/2)H(u)+((1-p)q/2)H(v), retained even for arbitrary hard cores. If each private controller has a strong certificate Hplus<=T(I-Var), the path has constant max(C0,T_A,T_B) against Q_s+Q_A+Q_B. If both cores have terminal constants L_u,L_v, the complete root cost uses max(C0,T_A,T_B,L_u,L_v). Ordinary controller certificates instead give K_A+C0 and K_B+C0. These are conditional quantified transfers, not inferred arbitrary-core certificates.

The first unsupported universal line is the nonempty signed collision when the two actual branch data cubes overlap, together with a universally admitted terminal policy. At shared data, the separate nonempty coefficients used in the selector spectrum add BEFORE squaring. Our proof does not bound that term by the same controller influence or erase H(u),H(v). The remaining problem is thus localized more sharply than a blanket ownership objection: the common control bit and mean-only collision are paid; nonempty branch collision and arbitrary terminal FEI remain unpaid.

# A057: coherent logarithm paid; scaled outer erasure remains

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Seek a nonreusing sum of the exact coherent-composition remainder across an actual physical restriction hierarchy. This stage proves two scoped all-dimensional statements: an exact erasure allocation identity with a full remainder upper bound, and a physical-tree payment for the retained logarithmic component. The full remainder-transport target remains open. Every main formula below is in nats; divide entropy quantities and constants by ln(2) for bits. Influence uses the ordinary uniform-cube convention.

For F=g(h_1,...,h_m) on disjoint blocks, all h_j balanced, write g=a+y_j b, q=Inf_j(g), alpha=hat h_j({i}), x=alpha^2, and Eraw(v)=sum v_T^2 ln(1/v_T^2). The complete imported remainder is

R=q x ln(1/x)+Eraw(a)+x Eraw(b)-[Eraw(a+alpha b)+Eraw(a-alpha b)]/2.

Both coherent terms and the logarithm remain explicit. No individual-child spectrum is replaced by its incoherent average.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

- EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, read in full; README, CURRENT_STATUS, CLAIM_REGISTRY, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY, PACKET_STATUS entry A057, STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS B12 entry, and campaign plan A057 contract lines 870--874 and Brain54 bibliography entry. The B12 notes file was absent. BOARD.md was read at stage start; A056's disjoint-composition work was preserved as upstream.
- Supplemental CAMPAIGN, CLAIMS, COUNTEREXAMPLES, SEED_LEMMAS and NATIVE_MAPPING; relevant source records ISOSPECTRAL_SPLICING_CURATED sections 2--9 and LOG_ALLOCATION_CURATED sections 2--6. Existing campaign B05 and B19 reports were read for the exact local-refund obstruction and review. B09 report was read for bias-tax scope; none of its pending stronger claims is a premise here.
- Threshold 38-page text, Section 7 / Proposition 7.1 and Corollaries 7.2--7.4; the 29-page earlier text's corresponding Section 7. Percolation 30-page and other 31-page texts' introductions and entropy/influence conventions: background only, no percolation theorem used. These were focused relevant-section reads, not a claim of independent whole-manuscript audit.

It sharpens NEXT; no pending A056 claim is needed for the two proofs above.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete proof is COHERENT_REMAINDER_PAYMENT.tex. Here is its mathematical ledger.

1. Put A_T=a_T^2, B_T=b_T^2, s_T=A_T+xB_T. The exact remainder is L_j(x)+J_j(x), where L_j(x)=sum s_T h_nat(x B_T/s_T) and J_j(x)=E(s)-[Eraw(a+alpha b)+Eraw(a-alpha b)]/2. Orthogonality gives identical total squared mass M=1-q+qx for both coherent child vectors. Thus J is a raw equal-mass JS divergence, not an entropy of an admitted Boolean leaf.
2. The actual erasure identity is B_i(F)=q B_i(h_j)+L_j(x). Residual block-j nonempty sectors contribute q times the inner erasure; the empty block-j sector has masses A_T,xB_T and contributes L. The corresponding JS identity is JS_i(F)=q JS_i(h_j)+J_j(x). These formulas retain the coherent phase dependence of physical child spectra.
3. Concavity of -u ln u gives E(A+xB)>=(1-x)E(A)+xE(A+B), hence L_j(x)<=x B_j(g)+qx ln(1/x). JS-Hellinger gives J_j(x)<=2 sum min(A_T,xB_T)<=2qx. Therefore R<=x B_j(g)+qx ln(1/x)+2qx. Every zero and endpoint case is covered in the proof.
4. Boolean physical half-differences give |alpha|<=Inf_i(h_j). Calculus yields alpha^2 ln(1/alpha^2)<=(2/e)|alpha|. Since Inf_i(F)=q Inf_i(h_j), the explicit logarithm costs at most (2/e) Inf_i(F). The constant in bits is 2/(e ln2), about 1.0615; the proof uses its exact symbolic value. This is a payment in actual influence, not an O(alpha^2) replacement.
5. Any finite actual physical restriction tree has I(f)=sum_internal w_v Inf_{i_v}(f_v)+E I(leaf). Thus the logarithmic bank over represented nodes costs at most (2/e) I(f), without reusing the same physical query. Full remainder sum is at most sum w_v alpha_v^2 B_{j_v}(g_v)+(2+2/e) consumed influence. Nodes need certified balanced representations; biased descendants are not assumed to retain them.
6. At a single root, sum over physical coordinates to get B(F)<=sum_j q_j B(h_j)+sum_j W_1(h_j) B_j(g)+(2/e)I(F). This is an exact interface to outer erasure transport, not a depth-independent recursive closure.

The first unsupported universal line is a source-valid physical hierarchy bound on
E H(leaf)+sum_v w_v[q_v D_{i_v}(h_{v,j_v})+alpha_v^2 B_{j_v}(g_v)]
by an absolute constant times the original influence, together with actual represented biased descendants. No such line is proved. At alpha=1, R=D_j(g), so a universal local full-remainder estimate would contain the known false all-coordinate restriction claim. The necessary AND alpha-log counterexample is retained in ALPHA_LOG_CONTROLS.md, including its benign selected-coordinate ratio.

# A057 stage 2: the capped recentered activation bank is paid

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Entropies here are bits. Put p=2^-r, a=1-Eu, K=4+2/(e ln2). The cumulative capped current biased gate bank is at most K p I(u)+5rp a<=K I(F). The constant is approximately 5.0615; the exact symbolic value is used in the proof. A partial core-tree prefix has a separately conserved variance/control reserve. This is not a complete inner-and-terminal entropy payment and does not prove FEI.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md supplies the complete analytic argument. At a core node let m be the actual mean, v=1-m^2, beta the singleton coefficient, t=beta^2, and d its physical influence. The positive gate erasure is
M(s)=Phi((1-p+pm)^2,p^2s)+p(1-p)Phi((1-m)^2,s),
with Phi(s,t)=(s+t)h2(t/(s+t)). The capped envelope is C=min{M(v),(t/v)M(v)+pt log2(v/t)}; t=0 and v=0 give zero. This is A056's current induced-bias outer erasure, not a uniform gate erasure reused after restriction.

The proof shows Phi(s^2,t^2)<=2st in bits by a monotone ratio calculation. The shifted empty-control row obeys (1-p+pm)^2+p^2v<=1. In the second cap branch its p^2 t log2(1/v) term combines with p t log2(v/t), leaving p t log2(1/t)+p(1-p)t log2(v). The latter term is nonpositive. Thus
C<=4p(1-p)d+p^2(2r+1/ln2)t+p t log2(1/t).

The retained rarity term is bounded by [2/(e ln2)]p d. Mean-martingale conservation gives sum w t=Var(u)-E Var(core_leaf), while ordinary physical influence gives sum w d=Pi_I(u). Hence the prefix is at most
[4p(1-p)+p^2/ln2+2p/(e ln2)]Pi_I(u)+2rp^2 V_drop.

For complete core trees, V_drop=Var(u)<=2a and Pi_I=I(u), so the prefix costs at most K pI(u)+2rp a. The control suffix only visits an r-bit point defect with probability a/2. Every point-defect queried erasure is <=3 times that query's influence, so the suffix costs <=3rp a. This gives K p I(u)+5rp a<=K I(F). The prefix's terminal control reserve and the suffix's consumed control influence are accounted for together; the same budget is not reopened at each depth.

The exact erasure ledger retains the negative core empty-pair subtraction:
B_tree(F)=p[B_tree(u)-sum w Phi(m_v^2,t_v)]+sum w M_v(t_v)+expected suffix erasure.
The physical entropy ledger retains the analogous nonempty inner decrement plus actual terminal entropy for partial core trees. Their general payment is not inferred from the cap bound. With outer JS paid separately, the complete-tree inner decrement remains bounded by the explicit pH(u), which is FEI-strength for arbitrary u.

The first unsupported universal line is a common-budget payment of nonempty inner-decrement sums and terminal entropies under a universally valid physical selection policy. For overlapping events with two different cores, even the present two-multiplicity-class gate formula and ownership of control reserve must be rederived; no such extension is claimed. Fresh repeated point controls on a single core collapse to one activation and do not create a new mechanism.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md contains the full proof. For F=C u+W, CW=0, W^2=1-C and q=EC, Parseval gives sum ahat^2=1-qv, sum v chat^2=qv. Homogeneous binary-entropy concavity gives M(v)<=h2(qv). Insert this into the cap's second branch and retain both logarithms: qt log2(1/v)+qt log2(v/t)=qt log2(1/t). Thus cap<=c_data qd+q log2(1/q)t. The physical martingale sums t to exactly V_drop; each rarity term is paid once.

Indicator isoperimetry q log2(1/q)<=I(C)/2 is proved by slice induction. At an actual control edge changing selected cores j to k, V_j+V_k<=4 Pr[u_j!=u_k]; this also holds for constant output states. Consequently sum_j V_j I(C_j)<=4I_ctrl and the combined selector reserve <=2I_ctrl. This closes the overlapping controller cap for any finite exclusive partition, without adding nominal event accounts. Data-prefix costs telescope against actual data influence. The prior scalar N<=2qt/ln2 pays the coherent outer addition. The fresh point selector has q=p and its variance reserve is rp Var(u)<=2rp(1-Eu); adding the reviewed suffix price 3rp(1-Eu) yields 5I(F).

Shared-data outputs would also break the independent-core mismatch calculation. No inference removes these debts.

# A058 overlapping compositions: locally complete restricted theorem

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite outer arity m, number of shared gates r, binary incidence matrix A and uniform Boolean sign outer gate F, compose g_i=x_i product_j b_j(z_j)^A_ji, with one private bit per outer input and pairwise disjoint shared blocks. Close an entropy ledger for unbounded shared-input reuse, without using disjoint-child composition identities. Entropy is in bits including the empty atom. The full self-contained proof is OVERLAP_ENTROPY_LEDGER.md, and the theorem manuscript is OVERLAPPING_COMPOSITION_CLASS.tex. The exact result is H(f)=H(F)+sum d_j H(b_j), I(f)=I(F)+sum d_j I(b_j); collision posterior H(T|A1_T) is retained. This yields unchanged constant transfer and an unconditional H<=2I class based on at-most-two-bit gates. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2; README.md, CURRENT_STATUS.md, CLAIM_REGISTRY.json, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY.json, PACKET_STATUS.json and STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS.json; A058 contract in AGENT_ASSIGNMENTS.json; campaign plan FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex, particularly Shared-data composition and the B12 packet contract. Read earlier B05/B09/B19 reports for obstruction scopes, not as theorem dependencies. Read BOARD.md (2026-10-11 22:16 UTC snapshot); B12 STREAM_NOTES.md did not exist.

Read supplemental CAMPAIGN.md, CLAIMS.md (C015 parity lift, C030 false overlapping subadditivity, C035 fresh-selector baseline), COUNTEREXAMPLES.md, SEED_LEMMAS.md, NATIVE_MAPPING.md and packaged sources. In particular OVERLAPPING_PRODUCT_NOTE.md distinguishes translation-averaged entropy from a chosen coherent product; ERASURE_REDUCTION.md section 4 identifies C018; ISOSPECTRAL_SPLICING_CURATED.md sections 5-6 warns against missing parity cancellation. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete proof expands F first, uses b_j^2=1 to reduce repeated shared factors modulo two, then derives the actual coefficient fhat(T,S)=Fhat(T) product_j c_j,U_j(T)(S_j). Private support T separates outer labels. Only after this derivation is the entropy chain rule applied to the normalized squared law. This avoids the refuted overlapping-product subadditivity claim. The posterior law is p(T|u)=p_T/q_u. Its full contribution B satisfies H(q)+B=H(F), not H(q) alone. Every physical influence is Inf_xi=Inf_i(F) or Inf_zjk=d_j Inf_k(b_j), with a separate spatial-mask derivation. Conditional transfer has no constant loss.

Every two-bit gate has H<=2I by truth-table classification; disjoint products add. Thus products of these gates may be used as outer/shared components at any size. The all-k common nonlinear gate family has H(q)=1, posterior=2k-1, H=2k+1 and I=k+1/2, showing unbounded reuse and exact equality. Even outer parity cancels the common gate completely. All proofs at the stated hypotheses are complete. The first unsupported line for removing a private anchor is a proposed influence payment for the resulting signed coherent fiber sums; no such estimate is claimed. Bent-splice selector-only increment bounds are not used: this proof keeps outer and shared data influence throughout.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Write a_T=Fhat(T), h_T=product of odd shared incidences and v_T(S)=a_T hhat_T(S). Private frequency phi identifies the first two membership bits by XOR; partners are T and T xor {1,2}. Actual coefficient c=v_T+v_partner. The latent law ell=v^2 pushes to Q=v^2+v_partner^2; actual P=(v+v_partner)^2=Q+delta. Both laws normalize: sum delta=E(h_1 h_2)sum_T a_T a_partner=0 by outer Booleanity. Hence H(P)=H(old)-B_lat-K+R, B_lat=H_ell(T|Y), K=KL(P||Q), R=-sum delta log2 Q. P<=2Q proves 0<=K<=1; posterior fibers of size two give 0<=B_lat<=1.

The bent-core family uses F=-1 only at (+1,-1), children xb_k and -xb_k, and b_k a product of k quadratic two-bit bent gates. Exact values: H(old)=k+2, H(new)=2k, R=k, B_lat=K=1, I_anchor=1, old I_data=k/2 and new I_data=k. Thus positive entropy increment k-2 and R cannot have any finite dimension-independent anchor-only price. A separate identical-literal child control has actual constant output, posterior 1 bit and anchor influence zero, refuting even the literal posterior-only price. This does not refute FEI: the bent family's H/I=2k/(k+1)<2. X004 is replayed as actual xy, H=0, I=2; X009 replays actual bent point splices and their selector influence, without presenting their existing asymptotic as new work.

The correct nonreuse interface counts the lost private pair: Delta I=sum delta(Y)(|phi(T)|+|S|)-L, L=2Pr_ell(both first labels selected). For D_C=CI-H,

    Delta D_C=C Delta I+B_lat+K-R.

The exact first unsupported universal line is a physical, ancestry-valid payment of positive G_C=R-B_lat-K-C Delta I with a conserved terminal deficit. Charging the full new data influence again would reuse the old budget. On the bent family G_2=-2 and on the zero-anchor constant control G_2=0; neither control refutes this properly retained deficit mechanism. The independent direct original/merged Walsh checker also certifies this influence-change identity on 4,224 cases with 75,009 exact assertions in 34.785s; no new universal sign or stopping theorem is claimed.

REVIEW_A057.md independently reconstructs the current cap: combine p^2t log2(1/v) with pt log2(v/t) before discarding the negative variance term; conserve sum w beta^2 and sum w physical influence separately; reserve rp(1-Eu) control influence once. Every complete fresh point-control core-first tree has capped-bank cost <=(4+2/(e ln2))pI(u)+5rp(1-Eu)<=(4+2/(e ln2))I(activation). This HOLDS at source scope; nonempty inner decrement and terminal entropy remain unpaid. No repair was supplied, so A058 is an independent reviewer of that version.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md supplies every all-dimensional step. The central statements are:

1. For balanced Boolean h, Lambda(h)<=(4+6/e)(I(h)-1). Choose the largest
   singleton coefficient a, let u=1-a and s be the other influences.
   The inequalities delta>=s-u and delta>=u(2-u)-(1-u)s imply
   u<=2delta and s<=3delta. The chosen term is <=2u; all other terms
   are <=(2/e)s. This is a proof with constants, not numerical evidence.
2. On a balanced composition tree, the exact stopping-frontier identity is
   delta(F)=sum_internal J(v)delta(g_v)+sum_terminal J(l)delta(Sub_l).
   Thus the local gate log bank is <=(4+6/e)delta(F), less any terminal
   reserve. Arbitrary refinement is monotone for each finite root.
3. If A(v) is the product of ancestor singleton coefficients squared, then
   Lambda(F)=sum_internal A(v)sum_i alpha_vi^2 ln(1/alpha_vi^2)
   W1(Sub_vi)+sum_terminal A(l)Lambda(Sub_l). Each edge introduces its
   logarithm once. A(v)<=J(v) and W1<=1 justify the gate upper bound.
4. Let G_r(x,y,z) select y only on the all-+1 event of r fresh controls,
   otherwise x. Put p=2^-r and F_t=G_r(F_(t-1),y_t,z_t), F_0=x_0.
   Every input block and child output is genuinely independent and balanced.
   I(F_d)=1+r[1-(1-p)^d]. The x_0 singleton coefficient is (1-p)^d.
   For d=2^(2k), r=3k, k>=2, the sum over nonroot continuation nodes is
   at least (1/2)2^k(1-2^(-2k)), while I(F_d)<=5/2 and tends to 1.
   Therefore no finite K pays the whole-subformula bank. These finite
   functions have dimension 1+d(r+1), depth d, and selector energy density
   p tending to zero. Exact logarithm and Bernoulli inequalities prove the
   divergence; no truth table of the exponentially large family is built.
5. The same family has H(F_d)=Q_r[1-(1-p)^d]/p in bits, with
   Q_r=(1-p)^2 log2(1/(1-p)^2)+2rp(2-p)<=6rp.
   Thus H(F_d)<=6(I(F_d)-1), uniformly in depth. This is a calibration
   of the repaired charge, not a new claim of generic read-once closure.

The exact first unsupported universal line is replacing A057's actual-query
log/outer-erasure bank by the LOCAL gate bank under physical restriction,
with recentered biased descendants and the same influence reserve. Formula
ancestor multipliers J and physical reach probabilities w are not identical.
No assertion that the new gate bank already upper-bounds that physical bank
is made. The counterfamily is a failed-helper certificate, not an FEI disproof.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

A controller query preserves U in expectation and releases kappa=sum q_j V_j D_2(P(X|C_j)||fair)>=0. A data query in core j consumes exactly q_j log2(1/q_j)beta_j^2 from R. Therefore R_root=E R_terminal+sum data losses+sum controller kappa for every finite stopping tree, regardless of interleaving or depth.

The signed inner term in A056's biased split is exactly U-E U_child-q_j eta(beta_j^2). A057's cap inequality contains the positive q_j eta(beta_j^2). This removes the 2/e surcharge for the FULL SIGNED DATA step, not for the standalone positive cap. Initial reserve funding R_root<=2I_ctrl follows from indicator isoperimetry and actual independent-core mismatch edges.

The theorem's full entropy inequality keeps the exact signed controller residual sum w(D_ctrl-kappa) and the actual terminal H,U,R corrections. The SAME physical influence telescope retains terminal influence. The reserve is funded once; neither controller residual nor terminal entropy is silently paid again from 2I_ctrl. # complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md supplies complete analytic proofs and COMPOSITION_SHARPNESS.tex
is the compact mathematical deliverable. New sharpness detail:

* For p=2^-r, q=1-p, Q=q^2 log2(1/q^2)+2rp(2-p),
  b_r=phi(q^2,p^2)+2p-2p^2, the chain has
  H=Q(1-q^d)/p, I=1+r(1-q^d), B=r b_r(1-q^d)/p.
  The continuation outer-erasure residual L is exactly zero; a concavity
  relaxation nevertheless adds D=sum q^(d-t)Lambda(F_t).
  D>=p d(d-1)q^(2d). With r=3k,d=2^(2k), actual H,B tend to zero,
  I tends to one, while D diverges. The informal epsilon diagnostic needs
  a factor-r qualification: deficit epsilon=rp gives D~epsilon d^2/r,
  H~4epsilon d; switch probability epsilon=p gives D~epsilon d^2,
  H~4r epsilon d. A059's explicit formulas already distinguish them.
* For selector probability p=2^-a and bent-point probability delta=2^-m,
  exact coefficients, H, means, correlations, B_W, influence split and
  selector/data chi-square are given in PROOF.md (7)--(8).
  The all-parameter rigorous enclosure is
  L-32p delta/(a ln2)<=B_W/I_W<=L,
  L=2(1-p)[m+2a-2+1/ln2]/a.
  This proves fixed-a divergence and quantifies its disappearance when
  a grows faster than m. The a=1 chi-square is exactly X009's value.
  I_data=m(1/2+2p delta) is retained; the example does not refute FEI.
* Fixed additive overhead is erased on a tensor-closed class. Per-factor
  overhead and a class lacking tensor closure require different claims.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md contains all analytic steps and the reduction of finite
logarithmic inequalities to integer comparisons. Main conclusions:

1. Lambda/(I-1) has maximum 3ln2 on all balanced n=3,4 and all odd n=5
   functions. The complete n=4 census has 224 equality cases: 32 signed
   Maj3 juntas and 192 signed/permuted variants of a four-input selector
   that returns x3 if x3=x4, otherwise x1 when x3=+1 and x2 otherwise.
   The latter has singleton coefficients (1/4,1/4,1/2,1/2), I=3/2,
   H=3 bits and Lambda=(3/2)ln2. Thus Maj3 is not the unique equality
   function. An independent implementation reconstructs the whole n=4
   census and equality orbit classification. The sharp finite bound
   improves the LOCAL per-gate bank coefficient from 4+6/e to 3ln2 for
   balanced arity<=4 or odd arity=5 gates, retaining the terminal deficit
   reserve. No physical biased-descendant payment is inferred.
2. Let d=2^-m, m even, b quadratic bent, D=1_(y=0) and
   g_m(z,y)=D+z(b-D). This exchanges the selector frequency sectors in
   X009. Its selected outer influence is 1-d, not d. Its packet masses
   are A_T=d^2 and V_T=(sign_T sqrt(d)-d)^2. For 0<x<1,
   0<B_j(g_m)-L_j(x)<=d log2(1/x). For even m>=4, B_j(g_m)>=dm;
   hence L/B>=1-log2(1/x)/m. The ratio tends to 1 at every fixed
   x>0, and even when alpha=2^-ceil(log2 m)->0. The construction
   h(s,w)=s v(w), with Ev=alpha, realizes the coefficient using balanced
   actual Boolean inners; physical queried B_s is exactly L(alpha^2).
   This refutes any uniform strict multiplicative cap improvement based
   only on a fixed singleton gap. At x=1/4,m=100, L/B>=49/50 by an
   analytic exact bound; no large truth table is constructed.
3. The cap coefficient 1 survives outer tensor products, because packet
   homogeneity and Parseval preserve each selected B and L, and sums
   over copies add. A fixed additive term cannot rescue a coefficient
   below 1. In contrast, Lambda of a product of two balanced functions
   is zero: it is not tensor additive. One cannot erase an additive term
   in a singleton-Lambda inequality using ordinary tensor powers.

The first unsupported sharp-singleton line is the inequality
Lambda(h)<=3ln2(I(h)-1) for every balanced h in arbitrary dimension;
even all balanced n=5 remains uncertified. The first unsupported
universal FEI line is a common-reserve payment of current biased outer
packet charges, nonempty inner decrements and terminal entropy. The cap
counterexample has ample data-side influence and does not refute FEI.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF.md defines actual F=b+(y-1)C, where b is paired quadratic bent and C a point indicator on r controls. Its Fourier sectors are sigma*sqrt(q)-q and q, with exact sign multiplicities. The y children are b and b-2C. Direct formulas yield D_y=qr+[T(2)/2-T(1)]/ln2, U_drop=0 and R_drop=qr. The convergent rational logarithm series proves

    (19/6)q/ln2 <= 3/ln2-(D_y-qr)/q
                         <= 8q/((1-4q)ln2).

Thus the signed DATA residual per spent influence approaches 3/ln2. The five-bit r=4 certificate already lies strictly between 4 and 41/10. The core beta=1 and beta^2=Inf_core=1, so replacing singleton squares by influence offers no discount. The actual terminal entropy is r-T(2)/(2ln2), and root influence is r(1/2+q)+q; H/I tends to 2, not infinity.

For disjoint products equipped with the additive per-factor ledger, the residual, reserve, influence spent and terminal entropy are additive. Tensorization therefore removes a fixed additive slack in that payment rule; a different canonical single-core representation or per-factor overhead is outside that conclusion. INDEPENDENCE_BOUNDARY.md supplies balanced distinct u=x and v=x(1-2A_t), nominal R=1 but I_ctrl=2^-t, with exact physical root and terminal costs. The missing covariance is identified explicitly. This does not refute the stated independent-core theorem or assert large collision entropy.
