# A061: fair adaptive restriction entropy process

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every n>=0 and Boolean sign f on the uniform cube, construct its entropy process along any finite fair adaptive physical restriction tree. Entropy uses bits, I is ordinary total influence. Prove exact conditional increments and the stopped tree identity, retaining terminal entropy. Identify the sign and a proved sufficient hypothesis for a compensated supermartingale. Full FEI remains OPEN. This is an A061 baseline/interface completion, not a claim of novelty or a universal payment theorem.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full (revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2), README.md, CURRENT_STATUS.md, CLAIM_REGISTRY.json, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY.json, PACKET_STATUS.json, STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS.json, and the campaign plan's B13/A061--A065 and acceptance-contract passages. The board snapshot is 2026-10-11 21:45 UTC; it lists A061--A065 at stage zero. claude/B13/STREAM_NOTES.md was absent at initial read; no direction is inferred from that absence.

Read supplemental CAMPAIGN.md, CLAIMS.md, COUNTEREXAMPLES.md, SEED_LEMMAS.md, NATIVE_MAPPING.md. Mathematical sources used: sources/ERASURE_REDUCTION.md sections 1--2 (exact erasure/JS identity and scalar bound), BLOCK_RESTRICTION_IDENTITY_2026-09-18.md sections 1--3 (channel and influence normalization), and LOG_ALLOCATION_CURATED.md sections 2--5 (rare logarithm and tree transport). Also inspected ISOSPECTRAL_SPLICING_CURATED.md and prior B05/B09/B19 run reports. The B05 original source is run 20261011T193057Z-fleet01-B05-rare-restrictions, sections 2--7, pinned in registry to commit 80cc2e5027efa56d79c4ecdf7d4949759ef2068b. The current paper context and earlier versions were accessed for introductory scope and restriction/composition passages; no threshold or percolation theorem is a premise in the proof below. Relevant paper names are threshold_paper_38pp.txt, threshold_paper_29pp_earlier_version.txt, percolation_spectral_pivotal_entropy_30pp.txt, percolation_spectral_pivotal_entropy_31pp_other_version.txt. Context source hashes and precise inspection record are in out/SOURCE_RECEIPTS.json when the metadata job completes.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Probability space and filtration

Sample X uniformly from {-1,1}^n. For randomized policies, also sample an independent policy seed R at time zero. Let F_t contain R, all coordinates queried before t and their physical values. The next coordinate i_t and the decision to stop must be F_t-measurable. Never query a coordinate twice. Conditional on F_t and continuation, X_i_t is a fair sign, and the remaining unqueried coordinates are uniform and independent. This follows inductively because a policy uses only revealed values and its independent seed. Let g_t be the actual Boolean function obtained by restricting f to the observed assignment. Let tau<=n be the stopping time. Freeze g_t and all processes after tau; all increments and charges below are zero there.

Write E_t=H(g_t), V_t=I(g_t), d_t=Inf_i_t(g_t). At a live node suppress t. Let a_T=ghat(T) and b_T=ghat(T union {i}) for T not containing i. Set q_T=a_T^2+b_T^2, theta_T=b_T^2/q_T on q_T>0. The child Fourier coefficients are a_T+sigma b_T, sigma in {+1,-1}. Let p_sigma(T)=(a_T+sigma b_T)^2. Boolean Parseval makes each p_sigma a probability law; their fair average is q. Thus d=sum b_T^2=sum q_T theta_T.

The spectral probabilities q_T and theta_T are not physical branch probabilities. At this one node one may introduce a fresh auxiliary experiment: sample sigma fairly, then Y with law p_sigma. It has marginal q, and J=I(sigma;Y)=H(q)-[H(p_+)+H(p_-)]/2. No global spectral subset is assumed to undergo the physical restriction.

### 2. Exact one-step increments

Let B=sum_T q_T h_2(theta_T), J as above, D=B+J. Splitting the parent spectral law into the removed bit and T gives H(g)=H(q)+B. Therefore

    D = H(g) - (H(g_+)+H(g_-))/2 >= 0.

Let eta_H=(H(g_+)-H(g_-))/2 and eta_I=(I(g_+)-I(g_-))/2. The exact realized increments, with sigma=X_i, are

    E_{t+1}-E_t = -D_t + sigma eta_H,t,
    V_{t+1}-V_t = -d_t + sigma eta_I,t.

For the influence identity, the average squared child coefficient at T is q_T, so average child influence is sum |T|q_T. Parent influence is that sum plus sum b_T^2. Fairness kills the sigma terms conditionally. In particular E_t and V_t are supermartingales; neither need decrease on every physical branch.

Define A_t=sum_{s<t}D_s and P_t=sum_{s<t}d_s (stopped charges). Then E_t+A_t and V_t+P_t are martingales. Their initial values are H(f) and I(f). They are integrable: 0<=E_t<=n, 0<=V_t<=n, 0<=D_t<=n, 0<=d_t<=1, and tau<=n. Summing finitely many conditional expectations gives

    H(f)=E H(g_tau)+E sum_{t<tau}D_t,
    I(f)=E I(g_tau)+E sum_{t<tau}d_t.

For a deterministic tree with node reach probability pi_v=2^{-depth(v)}, these are exactly H(f)=sum_internal pi_v D_v+sum_leaf pi_l H(g_l) and I(f)=sum_internal pi_v d_v+sum_leaf pi_l I(g_l). A randomized policy averages these identities over its seed. Terminal entropy cannot be dropped unless the leaves have zero entropy or it has a separate bound. Constant leaves have H=I=0; a terminal character can have H=0 and I>0.

### 3. A proved logarithmic compensation

Define the nonnegative predictable one-sided log charge

    lambda_t=sum_{T:b_T^2>0} b_T^2 log_2(q_T/b_T^2).

For theta in [0,1], -(1-theta)ln(1-theta)<=theta, since the derivative of theta+(1-theta)ln(1-theta) is -ln(1-theta)>=0. Consequently B_t<=lambda_t+d_t/ln 2.

Here is a self-contained channel bound. Put z=|2ab|/q in [0,1] for a positive packet. Its JS contribution is q d_nat(z)/ln 2, where d_nat(z)=((1+z)ln(1+z)+(1-z)ln(1-z))/2. The inequality d_nat(z)<=1-sqrt(1-z^2) holds: the derivatives are atanh(z) and z/sqrt(1-z^2), and the latter minus the former has derivative (1-z^2)^(-3/2)-(1-z^2)^(-1)>=0 and starts at zero. Integrate twice and extend to z=1 by continuity. Therefore the packet contribution is at most (q-|a^2-b^2|)/ln 2=2min(a^2,b^2)/ln 2. Summation gives J_t<=2d_t/ln 2. Combining,

    0<=D_t<=lambda_t+(3/ln 2)d_t.                 (* proved *)

Thus the explicit compensated quantity

    U_t=(3/ln 2)V_t-E_t-sum_{s<t}lambda_s

is ALWAYS a supermartingale on the stated finite trees: its conditional drift is D_t-(3/ln 2)d_t-lambda_t<=0. It is integrable: every nonzero Fourier coefficient of an actual restriction in at most n coordinates has magnitude at least 2^{-n}, q_T<=1, so every positive theta_T>=2^{-2n} and lambda_t<=2n d_t. The log cost is paid once, through its predictable cumulative sum. At stopping this yields only

    H(f)<= (3/ln 2)I(f) + E sum lambda_t
           + E[H(g_tau)-(3/ln 2)I(g_tau)].

The cumulative log term remains unpaid universally. Its presence is not an FEI proof.

### 4. Hypothesis with a proved finite influence price

Fix epsilon in (0,1]. Suppose at EVERY live node, every T with b_T^2>0 satisfies theta_T>=epsilon. Zero mismatch packets are exempt. Then lambda_t<=log_2(1/epsilon)d_t. This is a directly checkable spectral density hypothesis, not an assumption that the desired entropy drift is already bounded.

With C_epsilon=log_2(1/epsilon)+3/ln 2, the quantity

    Z_t=C_epsilon I(g_t)-H(g_t)

is a supermartingale: E[Z_{t+1}-Z_t|F_t]=D_t-C_epsilon d_t<=0. Equivalently H(g_t)-C_epsilon I(g_t) is a submartingale. Z_0=C_epsilon I(f)-H(f); at constant leaves Z_tau=0. For arbitrary stopping leaves,

    H(f)<=C_epsilon I(f)+E[H(g_tau)-C_epsilon I(g_tau)].

If terminal functions satisfy H<=C_epsilon I, this proves H(f)<=C_epsilon I(f). The conclusion holds in every dimension for any policy/tree satisfying this same fixed epsilon. No assertion is made that every Boolean function admits such a tree. Parities (queried theta=1) show the hypothesis is nonempty in unbounded dimension. As another simple sufficient condition, if every positive d_t>=delta, then B_t<=h_2(d_t)<=d_t[log_2(1/delta)+1/ln 2], and the same conclusion holds with C_delta=log_2(1/delta)+3/ln 2. These are conditional class statements, not new universal FEI results.

For a general F_t-measurable state potential Q_t, C V_t+Q_t-E_t is a supermartingale exactly when D_t<=C d_t+Q_t-E[Q_{t+1}|F_t]. This specifies the sign of the nonlocal debt needed by A064; it does not construct a universally paid Q.

### 5. Obstruction and first unsupported universal line

B05 gives actual Boolean splits with D>=B and B/d >= (1-2^{-k})(k-2) for every even k>=4. Hence no fixed finite C makes CI(g_t)-H(g_t) a supermartingale for ALL fair policies: start by querying that selector. This consumes the source-bound B05 control, rather than rerunning it or misreading it as failure of existential coordinate selection. The first unsupported universal step is: find an adaptive tree paying E sum lambda_t plus its terminal deficit by a dimension-independent multiple of I(f), or find a nonlocal Q whose boundary and conditional drift achieve that payment. The exact telescope alone does not supply it.

## executed validation with domain, arithmetic, commands and outputs

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The full proof is saved in POINT_CERTIFICATE.md. Writing d=2^{1-n}, the spectrum has empty mass (1-d)^2 and every nonempty mass d^2. A query has a constant child and a point-function child on n-1 variables. Thus every coordinate has the same rate R_n=(H_n-H_{n-1}/2)/d. The exact closed formula is (3) of that certificate.

The all-dimensional series comparison proves R_n strictly decreases for n>=3 and tends to 4. R_1=0, R_2=4, and the sharp maximum is R_3=12-(9/2)log2 3 in (4.867,4.868), attained exactly at dimension three. This is proved analytically for all n, not extrapolated from a finite table. In particular the dimension-four rate lies in (4.742,4.743), as the lead requested.

The exact one-sided log-charge rate is L_n=1-d+d log2((1-2d+2d^2)/d^2). Consecutive rates obey (9), and their sign is exactly the sign of the polynomial 1-4d. Hence the sharp maximum is L_3=L_4=(3+log2 10)/4 in (1.580,1.581); the tail strictly decreases from dimension four and tends to 1. Direct uniform-nu computation gives KL_2(nu_i||q_i)=n-1-L_n. Thus all coordinates of these rare functions recover their growing rarity logarithm, with a sharp dimension-independent unpaid remainder below 8/5.

All nonconstant physical descendants are point functions, so C I(g_t)-H(g_t) is a supermartingale for every fair adaptive rule in this family exactly at or above the sharp class price C=R_3. At C>=R_3, the lead's minimal nonnegative reserve Q* vanishes everywhere by backward induction. The n=3 node forces sharpness of that drift price. This does not claim that the sharp FEI constant H/I of the class is the same as its sharp drift price.

REVIEW_HANDOFF.md supplies source-bound review pointers for the lead's sandwich and KL form, consuming A087's existing independent acceptance of A061's baseline. A061 does not independently accept a chain containing its own proof contribution. No shared file or another agent's folder was changed and no message was sent.

First unsupported universal line: every arbitrary Boolean function has a physical coordinate with KL_2(nu_i||q_i)>=log2(1/d_i)-K for an absolute K, or an equivalent nonreusing stopping transport. The point proof uses equal nonempty spectral masses and a constant child; these disappear under signed multi-defect interference. Neither the exact polynomial calculation nor baseline conservation supplies the general statement.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PADDING_EDIT_PROOF.md contains the complete proof. For n=r+q, delta=2^-n and d=2delta, every newly relevant coordinate has influence d. Its exact paired packets are a_T=s d(u_T-1), b_T=-s d, where u_T is the signed old coefficient divided by d. Only K=|supp(ghat)| residual labels are occupied; all remaining labels have u=0. Thus the local ratios are baseline (lambda,B,D)=(1,2,4) plus explicitly signed occupied-packet corrections. Every formerly irrelevant coordinate has the same packet multiset.

The corrections are positive and bounded using Kd<=2^(1-q). In the notation of the proof,

    1 <= lambda_i/d <= 1+2Kd(n+1),
    2 <= B_i/d      <= 2+Kd(2n+4),
    4 <= D_i/d      <= 4+Kd(2n+7).

They tend to (1,2,4) for every fixed arbitrary core. The corresponding exact KL remainder is n-1-lambda_i/d. The new proof includes dimension-independent continuity of the one-sided conditional entropy lambda_i: its perspective is concave, and its Jensen gap is bounded by the binary mixture-label entropy. This complements the earlier B_i continuity and proves old lambda_i ratios converge as well. D_i continuity follows from the two binary conditional entropies B_i and J_i. Influences of old relevant coordinates change by at most 2delta, so their normalized ratios converge with an explicit denominator cutoff.

Consequently the edit operator acts on the three minimum relevant-coordinate ratios by the exact limits

    min_i B_i/d_i      -> min(2, min_old B_i(g)/d_i(g)),
    min_i lambda_i/d_i -> min(1, min_old lambda_i(g)/d_i(g)),
    min_i D_i/d_i      -> min(4, min_old D_i(g)/d_i(g)).

An empty old minimum is interpreted as +infinity. The conclusions are uniform in the choice of edited vertex at fixed core. The edited function has full Fourier support and H/I converges to the original core's H/I when I(g)>0. Thus the operation preserves the full-FEI hardness endpoint but cannot preserve an existential local witness above its respective cap. This generalizes A089's specific newly relevant coordinate warning to every Boolean core and adds the exact log-charge and entropy-drift operator limits; it is not a repeat of A002's full-support proof.

The all-added-coordinate stopping tree has exactly spent influence qd and terminal entropy (1-2^-q)H(g)+2^-q H(g^v), with the analogous terminal influence. The possible core deficit survives almost intact. This is why no root-only min-ratio admission proves the complete C019 bound.

The first unsupported universal line is a paid signed child/terminal reserve preserving a high local witness or carrying its residual core deficit after newly relevant coordinates are queried. Neither old-coordinate continuity nor the presence of full spectral support supplies that reserve. The exact terminal identity records the outstanding term rather than dropping it.

# A062 influence compensator

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every Boolean f on every finite uniform cube, and every fair adaptive physical restriction tree with independently randomized, history-measurable coordinate/block choices and stopping, prove I(f)=E(sum queried influence)+E I(terminal). Use exactly the same tree for the entropy telescope. First prove finite optional stopping; then justify randomized stopping, unbounded waiting, and any genuinely unbounded-process statement by explicit tail conditions. Influence is ordinary total influence, and spectral entropy uses bits.

**Result:** the finite-tree and fixed-dimension randomized-stopping interfaces are proved locally, including a separate conservation law for each original coordinate. The abstract unbounded-process extension requires uniform integrability of residual influence for equality, and a separate entropy condition. An actual Boolean activation family shows why bounded root budgets do not justify dimension-limit exchanges. Full FEI remains OPEN. This retires the baseline derivation after independent review; it does not claim new universal entropy payment or literature novelty.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, and the report README, CURRENT_STATUS, claim/obstruction registries, A062 packet entry, stream actions and campaign-plan restriction/B13 sections. Read BOARD.md at stage start. B13 STREAM_NOTES.md was absent on both checks, so the assignment and the protocol's independent-start provision supplied direction. No other agents' folders were modified.

Read the supplement CAMPAIGN.md, CLAIMS.md (especially C005, C006, C019, C023 and C024), COUNTEREXAMPLES.md (especially X001-X003 and X009-X011), SEED_LEMMAS.md and NATIVE_MAPPING.md. Relevant primary local derivations: sources/ERASURE_REDUCTION.md sections 1-4 and sources/BLOCK_RESTRICTION_IDENTITY_2026-09-18.md sections 1-3; sources/LOG_ALLOCATION_CURATED.md sections 3-5. The original B05 and B19 run REPORT.md files were read completely, preserving their no-go scope; the B09 REPORT.md was read with the registry's review/interval-repair objections and is not used as a proof dependency.

The percolation spatial forest is not identified with a physical restriction tree and none of its arm estimates are imported as universal facts. Only the relevant sections, not an independent audit of the whole manuscripts, are claimed here. Exact local hashes and paths are in out/SOURCES.json.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete proof is FAIR_TREE_INFLUENCE_PROCESS.tex; stopping-limit details and the analytic Boolean counterfamily are in STOPPING_JUSTIFICATION.md.

Conditioning on independent auxiliary randomness and revealed physical history leaves unrevealed input signs independent and fair. For a selected block A, edge averaging gives I(g)=I_A(g)+E_z I(g_z). On the same physical branch law, partial Fourier Parseval and the entropy chain rule give delta_A(g)=B_A(g)+J_A(g)>=0, where B_A is conditional spectral entropy and J_A is the one-node physical/spectral channel information. Thus I(g_t)+sum_{s<t}c_s and H(g_t)+sum_{s<t}delta_s are martingales. Multiply each one-step drift by the measurable indicator of continuing before the stopping time and sum finitely. This proves both stopped identities with terminal reserves. Edge averaging separately for each original coordinate gives its own conservation law, preventing label reuse.

A fixed block queried sequentially in any history-dependent order has expected total cost I_A(g); its path cost need not equal that number. Although I_A(g)=E_{S~p_g}|S intersection A| numerically, no unrelated spectral experiment determines the physical query law. A three-bit activated parity has root influence 3/2 and children of influences 0 and 2, refuting any interpretation as a pathwise decreasing wallet.

At fixed n there are at most n fresh queried coordinates. Cumulative influence cost is at most n, residual influence and entropy are at most n, and cumulative entropy drop is at most n^2 on each path. Truncate arbitrary randomized stopping at a finite time; bounded convergence proves the identities even with infinite expected waiting. For a genuinely unbounded process, monotone convergence proves integrability of cumulative nonnegative costs, but residual convergence yields only a Fatou inequality without uniform integrability. Entropy requires a separate condition and separate identification of its limit.

The Boolean dimension-limit witness uses g_k=y_0 times 2^{k-1} quadratic bent two-bit factors, with H(g_k)=2^k and I(g_k)=2^{k-1}+1. Activate it behind k controls, probability p_k=2^{-k}, and stop after the controls. Terminal influence and entropy converge almost surely to zero on coupled infinite control sequences, but their expectations tend to 1/2 and equal 1. Root influences are <=3/2. Thus bounded root budgets do not remove the terminal reserve in an all-dimensional limit. Every finite telescope remains valid; this is not an FEI counterexample.

For singleton trees, the proved scalar Jensen--Shannon inequality pays J_tree <= (2/ln 2) E(sum queried influence). A separate theorem B_tree <= K E(sum queried influence), plus certified leaf H<=C_L I, would therefore yield H(f)<=max(K+2/ln 2,C_L)I(f). **The first unsupported universal line is the existence of such a tree/policy with dimension-independent erasure payment and certified leaves.** No local fixed-price B_i<=K Inf_i is assumed; B05's counterfamily specifically excludes that repair. A064 may instead supply an explicit nonlocal debt potential, but its initial resource and terminal value still need proof.

## proof dependencies and changed downstream conclusions

The finite/stopping conservation proof is self-contained elementary edge averaging, conditional expectation and finite summation. # A062 stage 2: minimal reserve and isoperimetric credit

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

The B13 lead's v2 target is to decide whether an isoperimetric credit c mu log_2(1/mu) dominates Q* on the B05 selector-first tree for every parameter. We fix mu(g)=min(P[g=-1],P[g=+1]), the physical output minority, distinct from any spectral minority-fiber mass. Define Q*_v=[D_v-Cd_v+average Q*_children]_+, zero at constant leaves. The canonical actual Boolean family is the k-bit adjacent-pair quadratic bent function b_k, its flip b'_k at zero, activated by r point controls, and spliced at a fair physical selector. Domain: all even k>=4 and all integer r>=2k. The complete physical query rule is selector, controls, then adjacent data pairs, skipping irrelevant inputs. Entropy is in bits; influence is ordinary.

**Proved locally:** for every C>=6, including the lead's exact price 32609/5000=6.5218, all control nodes and even paired core entry states have Q*=0. Only the root and odd point-edited data states can have positive reserve. At all nodes Q*<=4mu log_2(1/mu), and at the root the stronger bound is Q*<4*2^{-k}mu log_2(1/mu)<=mu log_2(1/mu)/4. Consequently Q*_root<=2^{1-k}I(F). At r=2k and fixed C, Q*_root/I(F)~(2/5)2^{-k}. This directly resolves the lead's credit-domination question on the COMPLETE canonical tree. It does not assert that the rarity function itself has the required local drift, nor that every selector-first family has this cancellation. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete all-parameter proof is DERIVATION.md. The tree and the output-minority convention are fixed there before the estimates.

First, for an arbitrary Boolean core activated by s fresh controls, set x=2^{-s}(1-E core). The exact control erasure is B=2x-2x^2+Phi((1-x)^2,x^2). A two-range elementary entropy estimate proves B<=3x, and the accepted JS scalar inequality gives D<6x for nonzero costs. Thus every control node is paid at price six as soon as its terminal core reserve is zero.

Second, classify all data descendants produced by the adjacent pair order. Clean quadratic factors and characters are locally paid at rate four. Even point-edited states E_t have an odd edited child O_{t-1} and a clean child; the odd state's next partner query has children E_{t-2} and b_{t-2}. The exact edited-spectrum chi-square is 16*2^{-t}(1-2^{-t})<1 for t>=4, so Jensen gives H(E_t)>t-1. This controls D_O<=3/2 and D_E<=9/4 when t>=6. The odd reserve is at most 3/2; its half-weight plus the even entropy drop is at most three, while the even query spends 6(1/2+2^{1-t})>3. The t=4 case uses E_2's exact parity spectrum and has a margin at least 1/4 bit. This proves zero reserve at every even paired entry by induction, while keeping all odd positive branches. Their physical minorities lie in [3/8,1/2], so their reserve is bounded by 4mu log(1/mu).

It follows that every activation descendant has zero reserve. Hence the COMPLETE tree root reserve is exactly [D_selector-Cp2^{-k}]_+. The root entropy bound D<=d[log(1/d)+3/ln2], together with the actual physical minority mu=p(1-2^{-k/2}+2^{-k})/2, gives Q*_root<4*2^{-k}mu log(1/mu). The cube isoperimetric inequality I>=2mu log(1/mu) is proved by an elementary boundary induction in the derivation, yielding the initial reserve bound Q*_root<=2^{1-k}I. Constant leaves have zero entropy and reserve. The secondary class corollary is H(F)<=(6+2^{1-k})I(F)<=49I(F)/8. This corollary is not the claimed universal movement; the movement is the exact minimal-reserve location and nonreusing paired-query cancellation.

The candidate 4mu log(1/mu) STILL fails as a local debt potential on B05 selector roots for large k, exactly as A064 showed. Domination of Q* does not imply the right direction of its candidate drift after averaging children. The true Q* itself satisfies the Bellman drift and has a proved cheap root value on this rule.

**First unsupported universal line:** prove the same initial reserve bound for a physical rule on arbitrary Boolean sources, or supply a cheap explicit candidate closed under the Bellman update when two incompatible edited branches survive. The single aligned edit classification is not available in that setting. No universal terminal admission or arbitrary-core entropy bound is presumed.

# A062 stage 5: the controller owner at an unfinished-core frontier

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Let a be any Boolean controller seed, T any prescribed finite complete physical seed tree and C>=4. Tag it by a fresh sign w, and use h=w*a to select between two disjoint balanced parity cores. The initial reserve, including that prefix, is [Q_C(a,T)-pC]_+/2. Each prefix query spends exactly its own C/2 account once. Entropy is in bits; influences are ordinary. No bound on the seed reserve is assumed.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md proves the transfer by actual disjoint Fourier sectors: at every seed-query history H(F)=2+H(a)/2, Inf_i(F)=Inf_i(a)/2 and D_i(F)=D_i(a)/2. The tag keeps selector probabilities at 1/2; both parity cores remain balanced after the prefix. The lifted terminal w-mux has D=2,d=1/2 and zero reserve at C>=4. Finite Bellman induction therefore proves the exact half-transfer, retaining all terminal entropy. Prefix data queries have D=0,d=1/2 and identical continuation reserves, giving the exact clipped debit [Q_C(a,T)-pC]_+/2.

For the canonical B05 selector seed on k bent bits and r=2k controls, the physical selector packets give D_s/d_s>=(1-2^-k)(k-2). The lift preserves this rate at the first seed-selector query after a partial data reveal. Its variance release is exactly zero and parity-core reserves are zero. Thus the proposed arbitrary-controller extension D<=11d+reserve release fails for every even k>=14. This identifies the missing owner as the controller seed's COMPLETE reserve Q_C(a,T)/2; it neither forbids earlier prefix payment nor rules out another physical policy.

First unsupported universal line: price the inherited controller seed reserve from an exclusive unused source account, or replace the declared policy with one whose retained controller/core reserves have such a certificate. For unequal non-flat actual cores the half-transfer is unproved; coherent rows and both terminal references remain. This is a finite-tree theorem and a separate analytic all-k witness, not a passage to infinite-depth stopping.

# A062 shared-data influence compensator

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite Boolean seed a(z), every pair of Boolean cores u(x),v(x) on one common data cube, and fresh independent sign w, put M=(u+v)/2+w(u-v)/2 and F=(u+v)/2+w*a(u-v)/2. Entropy is in bits and influence ordinary. Transport nonnegative priced own-history reserves for a and M through finite adaptive interleavings of their declared trees. Shared coordinates are queried once; w may be queried only when the seed tree reaches its zero-reserve constant leaf. No bound on arbitrary inherited reserves is assumed.

Result: the exact coefficient is actual joint disagreement t=Pr[u!=v], including unequal non-flat shared cores. The graft R=t*r_a+r_M is priced at max(C_a,C_M); its controller term has zero drift under shared-data queries. At equal prices, Q_C(F)<=t Q_C(a)+Q_C(M). This scoped extension is locally proved, pending independent review. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md supplies the complete proof. The fresh tag separates the b=(u+v)/2 and c*a=(u-v)*a/2 Fourier sectors, yielding H(F)=H(M)+t H(a), for arbitrary shared-core spectra and biases. Seed queries have D_F=t D_a and d_F=t d_a. At a data query, t is a conditional probability of the same actual disagreement event and is harmonic; consequently D_F=D_M and d_F=d_M=(d_u+d_v)/2. Backward induction with the same inherited reserves proves the nonnegative graft, complete least-reserve bound and finite stopped budget with both actual terminal H and R retained. No limit or unbounded-depth passage is taken.

An explicit unequal three-bit shared pair has u negative only at0 and v negative at0,2. At data bit0, actual t=1/8, children t=1/4,0, hence zero reserve release. The independent-core proxy (1-Eu Ev)/2 is5/16, with child mean1/4, creating false release1/16. Its general spurious drift is beta_u beta_v/2. This refutes a shared-data extension of that substitution, not A030's stated independent-block theorem.

The false release can be arbitrarily large for a supplied complete controller reserve: the three-bit point-defect seed has first-query debt ell=D-4d>1/8, rigorously enclosed in out/EXACT_SHARED.json. Tensor L such seeds and query one untouched coordinate in each before completion. Fourier-product identities and the Bellman inequality give Q_4>=L ell. The counterfeit release is at least L ell/16 while the actual controller release remains0. This is a bookkeeping obstruction, not an unbounded H/I family.

The first unsupported universal line is paying the tag-exposed collision with the same remaining controller/profile reserves and both actual child terminal references. This stage supplies neither a uniform initial reserve bound nor a universal policy avoiding that bill.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The full derivation is in QUERY_INFORMATION_IDENTITY.tex. Here are its mathematical steps and their dependencies.

1. For block A and residual Fourier index w, let a_{u,w}=fhat(u union w), c_{x,w}=sum_u a_{u,w}chi_u(x), and q_w=sum_u a_{u,w}^2. The actual joint physical/residual experiment has P(X_A=x,W=w)=2^-|A| c_{x,w}^2. Boolean Parseval gives a uniform physical marginal; partial Walsh Parseval gives residual marginal q. The spectral parent conditional law is nu_w(u)=a_{u,w}^2/q_w and the physical posterior is r_w(x)=2^-|A|c_{x,w}^2/q_w. Therefore B_A=sum q H(nu)=H(S_A|S_C), J_A=|A|-sum q H(r)=I(X_A;W), and H(f)-E H(f_x)=B_A+J_A. In the packet's collision notation B_W=B_A and M_W=J_A. Physical outcome entropy |A| is a different quantity.

2. The experiment does not specify how the deleted parent label U is coupled to the branch X_A. Every extension with the correct (U,W) and (X_A,W) marginals has I(U,W;X_A)=J_A+I(U;X_A|W). The canonical A037 lift imposes conditional independence and attains the minimum J_A. The three-bit point defect in PHYSICAL_SPECTRAL_DISTINCTION.md gives two exact nonidentical lifts with unchanged physical/residual marginals; the additional information in the second is strictly positive by nonindependence and Gibbs. This demonstrates why a kernel is required before assigning full-label information.

3. Independently reconstruct A037's finite adaptive common lift. Starting with root S~fhat^2, maintain current S_h=S intersect R_h. At node h draw outcome x with kernel r_{h,w}(x), w=S intersect (R_h\A_h), and erase A_h from the current set. Induction gives actual fair physical branches and actual conditional child spectra. Since the kernel depends on the root S only through w and history, I(S;next outcome|h)=J_h. For stopped transcript L, sum p_h J_h=I(S;L) and H(S|L)=sum p_h B_h+E H(g_L). These are baseline identities already proved by A037 and are independently accepted here at this finite-tree scope. They measure actual information under the stated coupling; they do not erase root posterior uncertainty when current coordinates disappear.

4. New fixed-block coarsening refund. Fix A and query all and only its coordinates, possibly choosing their order from physical history. For deterministic policy the full transcript is in bijection with X_A. The final joint (X_A,S_C) law equals the actual direct-block experiment: each leaf has uniform probability and the actual child spectrum. Under the sequential lift put R_A=I(S_A;X_A|S_C). The chain rule gives sum p_h J_h=I(S;X_A)=J_A+R_A. The total entropy decrement is order independent, so sum p_h B_h=B_A-R_A. Equivalently sum p_h B_h=H(S_A|S_C,X_A). Thus 0<=R_A<=B_A. The direct-block lift has R_A=0 by construction; singleton refinement can reveal erased spectral information and reduce residual erasure. The sum is invariant; its two pieces can depend on query order. This is a conditional-information identity, not an influence-payment theorem.

5. Reference audit. For normalized supported child references Q_x, mixture Q=E Q_x and posterior t_w(x)=2^-a Q_x(w)/Q(w), the joint KL chain rule gives E D(P_x||Q_x)=D(q||Q)+sum q D(r_w||t_w). Moreover sum q D(r_w||t_w)=J_A+E log2(2^-a/t_W(X_A)). The last term is signed and cannot be suppressed unless its cancellation is proved. This is the precise local reference/mixing correction; A037's general channel/reset ledger applies across incompatible node references.

The refund has a sharp quantitative ceiling. The self-contained positive-series proof of h2(t)>=4t(1-t), applied to the erased-bit and branch posteriors in each singleton fiber, gives J_h<=B_h. Hence R_A<=(B_A-J_A)/2. For any canonical singleton stopping tree, I(S;L)<=[H(f)-E H(terminal)]/2 and H(S|L)>=[H(f)+E H(terminal)]/2. Two-bit AND attains the ceiling with direct block charges (2,0), sequential charges (1,1), and refund 1; disjoint AND products show sharpness in arbitrarily large dimensions. Thus paying only released root information leaves at least half the original entropy unpaid. This is a coupling-mechanism barrier, not an FEI refutation.

6. Controls. Isospectral children give J=B=0 even when the physical branch is a fair bit and the functions differ. For nearly identical child squared spectra P_+,P_-, define m=(1-sum sqrt(P_+P_-))/2. The parent minority fiber mass sums to m, so B<=h2(m); the baseline Hellinger inequality gives J<=2m/ln2. Both vanish with m, retaining the logarithmic scale of B. Exact point-defect expressions r=2..8 are preserved by the checker. None of these statements pays a universally large rare-erasure bill by one local influence.

The first unsupported universal line is a dimension-independent bound for H(S|L)=sum p B+E H(terminal), under a constructive query policy whose physical influence is charged once. Paying J again, or dropping its kernel/reference correction, cannot provide that bound. The fixed-block refund identifies a potential mechanism but does not establish sufficient refund on arbitrary Boolean sources.

For two queries the first kernel depends only on the second spectral bit and the untouched residual; the second outcome is conditionally independent of root labels given the first outcome and residual. The conditional mutual-information chain rule gives the two-query formula.

The actual family has six coefficient classes and only one exceptional (empty) residual packet. In all other packets the 2x2 amplitude matrix has identical columns: `[[C_T,C_T],[d,d]]`. Either order therefore reveals zero information about the remaining block spectral bit. The central matrix is `[[1+C,C],[d,d]]`, C=-p(1-s+delta). For both order channels its raw conditional Bernoulli variance is at most 2d^2, and its outcome mean lies in [1/4,3/4]. The elementary KL/chi-square inequality gives the stated upper bound; opposite row probabilities prove strict positivity.

The selector bound is `B_y>=(1-delta)(k-2)d`. Consequently `R_A/B_y<=16d/((1-delta)(k-2))` tends to zero. For even k>=6 the accumulated MU ceiling is at least (15/32)d. A universal price `B_y<=K*d+M*R_A` for fixed finite K,M>=0 is thus false on actual Boolean functions. The same conclusion does not rule out paying the two-query block from control influence (which is much larger), more queries, or existing stopping certificates.

No unsupported line occurs inside this narrow theorem. The first unsupported universal line is that, after an actual mixed data/controller history, a named unused inherited conditional child/control slack pays the signed nonmatching reference conversion together with both complete retained terminal laws at a dimension-independent influence price. Information refund by itself provides no such bank.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A064 / B13, global debt potential, assigned run 20261011T224431Z-fleet05-A064-global-debt-potential. All functions are Boolean signs on finite uniform cubes; Fourier coefficients are normalized; entropy, KL and logarithms are in bits. Total influence is ordinary physical influence. A fair physical restriction tree queries only fresh coordinates, with decisions determined by revealed history and an independent seed, and stops within n reveals. The actual Boolean restriction at a node is g_v, the queried influence d_v, and its entropy decrement D_v=H(g_v)-avg H(children). Branch probabilities are physical, not spectral.

The global payment condition has the sign convention

    D_v <= C d_v + Q_v - E[Q_child | v].

The first deliverable is a boundary-complete finite-tree payment theorem. The explicit successful potential is the terminal-corrected reserve

    Q_v = H(g_v) - E[H(g_terminal) | v].

It has exact drift D_v, nonnegative values and zero terminal values, but can grow on a rare branch. Its initial resource bound is proved independently for arbitrary-core point activations, Q0<=8 times the exclusive queried control influence, retaining the full arbitrary terminal entropy. On the unbounded family of activated arbitrary bent point-splice cores, both control-first and selector-first initial bounds are proved, respectively <=8 and <=9 times queried influence, with separately certified terminal entropy below 3 times terminal influence. These give class certificates H<=8I and H<=9I; the second is weaker than A023's direct H<=8I result and is a certificate for this potential and tree order, not a new better FEI constant.

The three lead-prescribed candidates are tested at actual selector roots: c*eta log(1/eta), c*I_remaining log(1/pi), and CI-H. The first two have all-fixed-constant no-gos for their stated formulas. The third has a sign obstruction and fails even when parent and children already satisfy the chosen FEI constant. None of these failures is a complete-tree total-debt falsifier or an FEI counterexample.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## Payment theorem and terminal correction

Let B=E sum d and L_I=E I(terminal) on the same finite tree. If an adapted integrable Q satisfies the displayed payment condition, E Q0<=K B, and

    E[H(terminal)-Q_terminal] <= L L_I,

then

    H(f) <= (C+K) B + L L_I <= max(C+K,L) I(f).

Indeed sum_{s<t}(D_s-Cd_s)+Q_t is a supermartingale. Finite expectation summation gives E sum D<=CB+E Q0-E Q_terminal. Add the retained terminal entropy, and use the exact nonreusing influence split I(f)=B+L_I. The potential may be signed at intermediate nodes. A sufficient leaf condition is Q_terminal>=0 with H(terminal)<=L I(terminal). If the weaker root condition E Q0<=K I(f) is all that is proved, the corresponding conclusion is H<= [K+max(C,L)]I; it does not give the sharper queried-budget constant. This distinction prevents using a terminal influence account once for the reserve and again for the leaf.

For any fixed finite tree define A_v=E[H(terminal)|v], Q_v=H(g_v)-A_v. A is a martingale. Thus Q_v-avg Q_child=D_v, and Q_terminal=0. The erasure plus JS identity makes D_v>=0, so backward induction proves Q_v>=0. The same backward induction shows this is the unique exact-drift solution with zero leaves. Its construction is general, but Q0=H(f)-E H(terminal) is not automatically linearly paid. With complete constant leaves and a universal Q0<=KI, that initial assertion would be FEI itself. No such bound is assumed.

This is a remaining-bill reserve; it need not decrease pathwise. Its entire expected terminal value is exactly zero for the finite trees below. No dimension limit, uniform-integrability shortcut or almost-sure disappearance of unpaid core entropy is invoked.

## Exact spectral minority/KL bill and coherence

At a node let x_T,y_T be the two actual child Fourier coefficients, q_T=(x_T^2+y_T^2)/2, m_T=(|x_T|-|y_T|)^2/4, M=sum m_T, and nu=m/M for M>0. Exactly

    D = M log(1/M) - M KL(nu||q) + R_B + JS(x^2,y^2),
    R_B = sum_T (q_T-m_T) log[q_T/(q_T-m_T)].

Zero-mass terms contribute zero, including M=0. The two remainders satisfy R_B<=M/ln2 and JS<=2M/ln2. The first follows from -(1-t)ln(1-t)<=t; the second follows from the scalar comparison between the JS contribution and half the squared Hellinger distance, as reconstructed in A062 and the source ISO section 3. M<=d, but M need not equal the signed queried mass d. M is also different from the physical output minority eta tested below.

The reserve drift is this entire D. Therefore Q0 equals the physically weighted sum of these exact bills, with each node's mu log(1/mu) and own-reference KL rebate included once. No KL value from one node is credited to another node with a changed reference. Branch probabilities multiply complete bills and are never substituted for spectral packet probabilities.

For the arbitrary-bias activation proof below, coherent constant-frequency terms remain explicit: m^2 log(m^2), a^2 log(a^2), and (1-pa)^2 log((1-pa)^2). They are the actual squared Fourier coefficients after recombination, not entropy of an incoherent label mixture. This includes the requested alpha^2 log(alpha^2) endpoint terms.

## Explicit arbitrary-core activation reserve and initial bound

Let g be any Boolean core, m=Eg, a=1-m in [0,2], r>=1, p=2^-r, and F=1+A_r(g-1) with fresh point controls. Query controls until the first mismatch, or stop at g after r matches. At an active node with s remaining controls, u=2^-s, set

    Q_s = H(1+A_s(g-1)) - u H(g).

All inactive leaves and the final core have Q=0. Conditional survival to the core is u, so this is precisely the general terminal correction. Its drift is D and it is nonnegative. Exclusive queried influence is B_ctrl=rpa, while expected terminal entropy and influence are pH(g), pI(g).

The actual Walsh coefficients give, with 0log0=0,

    Q_r = 2rpa(2-pa) + p m^2 log(m^2)
          -(p-p^2)a^2 log(a^2) -(1-pa)^2 log((1-pa)^2).

This follows by separating nonempty data packets, empty-data nonempty-control packets and the empty coefficient; no mean sign is discarded. The m term is nonpositive. The final term is <=2pa/ln2. For a>=1 the a term is nonpositive; for 0<a<1 it is at most 2pa[a log(1/a)]<=2pa/(e ln2). Therefore

    Q_r <= [4+2(1+1/e)/ln2] rpa < 8 rpa.

The a=0 case is exactly zero. The last strict constant is proved by the rational lower bounds ln2>56/81 from its atanh expansion and e>65/24 from its exponential expansion; 2*(81/56)*(1+24/65)<4. Negative means, m=0, a=2 and the last-layer sign change are all included. This reconstructs A023 section 4, credited as the source direction; it is not a new claim about the activation entropy identity itself.

If g has an independently proved H(g)<=L I(g), payment gives H(F)<=max(8,L)I(F). Without it the exact pH(g) is retained and unpaid. The threshold paper's activation equivalence explains why arbitrary core entropy cannot be dropped merely because p is tiny.

## Both query orders on an unbounded bent-splice family

Let b be any bent function on even k>=4 bits, v any vertex, delta=2^-k, b'=b-2b(v)1_v, and g the fair physical selector between b and b'. Activate g with arbitrary r>=1 fresh controls. This is an unbounded family in both k and r; r=2k with quadratic b and v=0 is B05.

Bent flatness gives I(b)=k/2 and |Eb|=sqrt(delta). Point-flip edge counting gives Ibar=(I(b)+I(b'))/2>=k/2-kdelta>=7k/16; I(g)=Ibar+delta. Also H(g)<=k+1<=5k/4, so H(g)<=20I(g)/7<3I(g). The activation parameter a=1-Eg lies in [11/16,21/16]. Thus control-first has Q0<=8rpa, zero terminal reserve and terminal entropy <=3pI(g), yielding H<=8I.

For selector-first, first query the physical selector at the root, then use the above control-first trees in its two children. Leaves are constant, b or b'. Root d=pdelta. The signed Fourier allocation is lambda=d[log(1/d)-KL(nu||q)]<=d log(1/d); the full decrement is <=lambda+3d/ln2. Therefore

    D_selector <= pdelta(r+k+3/ln2) <= rpa.

For the second bound use delta<=1/16, kdelta<=1/4, r>=1, 1/ln2<81/56, and 1/16+1/4+243/896<11/16<=a. The actual root potential is D_selector+avg(Q_G,Q_H). Child reserves average to <=8rpa, so Q0<=9rpa<=9B, B=rpa+pdelta. The expected terminal entropy is <=pk; its expected influence is pIbar>=7pk/16, so it is separately certified below 3. This yields H<=9I on this order.

The selector bill and descendant control bill receive disjoint allowances of one and eight times B_ctrl, within the declared nine-times reserve. The terminal data account pIbar is separate. Control-first has no independent selector bill and keeps its entire entropy in the core leaf. The terminal correction accounts for this difference exactly. No coordinate's initial influence is silently pledged to two bills without the stated extra factor.

For products of disjoint components the same Q values add: Fourier entropy and influence add, conditional terminal entropy adds, and a query affects only its component. Thus these certificates retain unbounded component count without a depth factor. This extension is elementary tensor additivity, not a solution for shared controls.

## Candidate failures and first unsupported universal line

Full formulas, all-fixed-constant proofs and exact certificates are in POTENTIAL_OBSTRUCTION_TESTS.md. For c=1,C=4, both unactivated k=4 and B05 k=4,r=8 selector roots refute all three named candidates. The actual parent and both children satisfy H<4I in each test.

Physical-minority rarity has drift O(pdelta^2), while D>=(1-delta)(k-2)pdelta. Hence for any fixed c,C its drift cannot absorb B05's k*d term. The explicit history candidate Q=c I_remaining*depth has Q0=0 and average first-child potential c(I-d), so it gives a negative right side at the sparse selector for sufficiently large k when c>0. The c=0 case is the false local D<=Cd bound. These are failures of the specified candidates, not assertions about every nonlinear history potential.

CI-H has drift Cd-D and the requested inequality holds iff D<=Cd. This is the specific sign disagreement with the lead's third candidate. The opposite sign H-CI satisfies the exact drift equation but has an unpaid universal root bound. It does not provide an independent FEI proof.

In the B05 finite certificate, Q_root/(rpa) is in (4.38709653,4.38709654). On the first control match the actual reserve grows by a quantity in (0.08565966,0.08565967) bits, while the mismatch child has Q=0. The exact identity Q_root-avg Q_child=D proves the expected control despite the growing branch.

First unsupported universal line: there exists, for every Boolean f, a physical stopping tree whose terminal-corrected reserve has Q0<=K times its exclusive queried influence with a universal K and whose actual leaves have a uniform entropy/influence certificate. Neither the existence of such a tree nor a universal reserve estimate is proved here. Arbitrary-core activation pays only its control reserve and leaves pH(g) visible.

# exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

A064/B13, run 20261013T235917Z-fleet05-A064-global-debt-potential. Uniform finite physical cubes, Boolean signs, normalized Fourier coefficients, entropy and KL in bits. The target is a nonnegative reserve for actual stopped prefixes, not the tree-invariant total debt H-CI. The policy, continuation rules and stopping frontier are part of every statement.

New version-2 interface: an arbitrary finite control partition exclusively selects independent Boolean cores on disjoint data blocks, or constant signs. Events can share any physical control coordinates; multiple descriptions selecting the same physical core are merged first. During the prefix controls are unqueried. Each core follows its declared finite continuation tree and has its own nonnegative C-priced reserve r_j. Interleaving and stopping may be adaptive, but a core reserve depends only on its own history and initially fixed independent seed. No uniform bound for arbitrary r_j is an input.

The explicit potential is

    R_t = sum_j q_j r_j(remaining core)
          + sum_j q_j log2(1/q_j) Var(remaining core),

where q_j is the actual exclusive state probability. With alpha=C+11/2, it satisfies D_F<=alpha*d_F+R_t-E R_next, and

    R_0 <= sum_j q_j r_j(root)+2 I_ctrl(F).

It therefore dominates the minimal nonnegative alpha-priced data-prefix reserve under the prescribed rule. Expected partial terminal reserve is <=R_0+C times consumed data influence. For complete constant-core leaves it is exactly zero; their actual control-function entropy is retained. A nonnegative harmonic lift of the ACTUAL boundary controller reserve grafts a declared continuation without resetting its frontier, yielding

    Q_alpha(F,full rule) <= sum_j q_j r_j(root)
                          +2 I_ctrl(F)+E b_actual_frontier.

The boundary debit is not assumed paid. If separately paid at L times control influence, the control account is 2+L, not max(2,L). This is a potential and prefix-ownership theorem for arbitrary exclusive controllers, rather than a new isolated bent class. Shared data between distinct selected cores and control queries interleaved inside this prefix are outside its hypotheses.

# complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

## Independent ledger review and the nonnegative empty-row refund

For a data query of core j, let m=E u_j, beta=hat u_j(i), t=beta^2, v=1-m^2, q=q_j. The auxiliary Boolean fallback W satisfies C_j W=0 and W^2=1-C_j. Its actual signed mean-sector Fourier vector is a=hat W+m hat C_j, not an incoherent mixture of fallback amplitudes. With phi(s)=s log2(1/s) and Phi(A,B)=phi(A)+phi(B)-phi(A+B), the exact source identity is

    D_F = q[D_j-paird(m,beta)]+M(t)+J,
    paird(a,b)=phi(a^2)+phi(b^2)-avg phi((a+/-b)^2).

The independent main checker builds the actual full Boolean table and its child spectra, and separately constructs those controller rows from current means and singleton coefficients. It checks the exact identity and

    D_j-paird = E_ne(u_j)-avg E_ne(child)-phi(beta^2),
    E_ne(u)=H(u)-phi((Eu)^2).

Thus the actual stopped A087 ledger is accepted at its explicit hypotheses, including partial leaves. The complete proof is finite summation on the actual interleaved tree. Nothing permits swapping its frontier for the control-first terminal pi_j H(u_j).

The original source's projected-mass witness is independently reconstructed by direct character sums: masses1 and3/4 average to7/8, physical probabilities1/2,1/2, while normalized spectral weights are4/7,3/7. The strict mass correction is15/8+(3/8)log2(3)-(7/8)log2(7)>0 by2^15*3^3>7^7. The data-first terminal entropy11/8-(9/32)log2(3) exceeds the control-first3/4 by more than1/16. REVIEW_A087.md supplies the all-dimensional mass algebra and exact original fixture; this is a source-bound HOLDS verdict, not a new universal payment.

Crucially paird is nonnegative. Write it as Phi(a^2,b^2)+phi(a^2+b^2)-avg phi((a+/-b)^2). The first term is homogeneous binary entropy, and the second is nonnegative by concavity of phi. This permits dropping the refund in an upper bound; it never permits deleting it from the exact signed ledger. All zero endpoints use0log0=0.

## Local priced lift and one variance-rarity account

The selector envelope gives M(v)<=h2(qv) and

    M(t)<=t M(v)/v+qt log2(v/t)
         <=qt[log2(1/q)+1/ln2]+qt log2(1/t).

The v-log cancellation is exact. The actual unnormalized projected-sector gap satisfies0<=J<=2qt/ln2, including its mass correction. Since beta is the mean of a Boolean derivative, |beta|<=d_j and t<=d_j. Also t log2(1/t)<=2d_j/(e ln2). Therefore

    M(t)+J <= kappa q d_j+q log2(1/q)t,
    kappa=(3+2/e)/ln2<11/2.

The strict rational comparison uses e>65/24 and ln2>56/81: (3+48/65)*(81/56)<11/2. No floating constant estimate supplies the theorem.

Insert the supplied core reserve D_j<=C d_j+r_j-E r_j(child), discard the nonnegative paird only as an upper bound, and use Var(u_j)-E Var(child)=beta^2. The global data influence is d_F=q_j d_j. Other cores' own-history reserves and variances are unchanged by this query. The displayed R therefore satisfies D_F<=alpha d_F+R-E R_next on every node of the declared prefix.

Backward induction of Q_alpha=[D-alpha*d+E Q_child]_+ proves Q_alpha<=R, because R is nonnegative and dominates the specified zero leaf potential. This controls every stopped prefix, not merely the total bill of a completed tree. A030's order-sensitive examples therefore do not invalidate this particular declared-policy bound.

For the initial resource, probability-of-change indicator influence obeys I(C_j)>=2q_j log2(1/q_j). Its cube-slice proof uses h2(s)>=2min(s,1-s). At an original control edge switching states j,k, actual pivotal probability is (1-m_j m_k)/2 because the distinct cores are independent. Their variance sum is at most2(1-m_j m_k). Constants have variance zero; an edge staying in one merged physical state has no charge. Summing original edges once gives sum_j Var(u_j)I(C_j)<=4I_ctrl, hence

    sum_j q_j log2(1/q_j)Var(u_j)<=2I_ctrl.

This is a single exclusive control account, valid for arbitrary dimension, state count and overlapping nominal event descriptions. It does not require private literal widths or independent control events.

## Every boundary and the expected terminal value

At any finite partial stop sigma, the potential is exactly its displayed remaining-core sum. Summing each supplied core inequality gives

    E sum_j q_j r_j(sigma)
      <=sum_j q_j r_j(root)+C Pi_I,data
        -sum_j q_j E sum actual core D_j.

The last sum is nonnegative. Variances also decrease in expectation, so E R_sigma<=R_0+C Pi_I,data. This gives a controlled expected terminal value even when individual branch reserves grow. All arguments are on finite cubes with bounded fresh reveals; no optional-stopping or all-dimensional limit exchange is assumed.

When the supplied core trees exhaust each core to a constant with zero r_j leaves, R_sigma=0 exactly. The actual frontier F_sigma is a Boolean CONTROL function whose entropy and chosen continuation reserve can still be arbitrary. Let b_sigma dominate the alpha-priced reserve of that actual control continuation. A_t=E[b_sigma|history] is a nonnegative martingale on the data prefix; adding it leaves the drift unchanged and at the frontier gives exactly b_sigma. This grafts the declared controller rule with no changed frontier, incompatible spectral reference or new credit.

The root bound becomes sum_j q_j r_j(root)+2 I_ctrl+E b_sigma. If core reserves are separately <=K_j I(u_j) and actual frontier controller reserves are <=L I_ctrl(F_sigma), then E I_ctrl(F_sigma)=I_ctrl(F) by edge averaging, so the root is <=sum_j q_j K_j I(u_j)+(2+L)I_ctrl. The two controller capacities have been added explicitly. The same original control influence is not used at rate2 and then again free at rate L.

The retained KL rebate is compatible with this drift: at every full Boolean node D=M_spec log2(1/M_spec)-M_spec KL(nu||q_spec)+R_B+JS, where M_spec is spectral minority-fiber mass and R_B is the complementary binary-entropy half. The exact signed D used here contains all these terms. The proof bounds the coherent positive sector, retains the scalar paird refund as an explicit nonnegative term, and does not transfer a node's KL reference to another history. Physical q_j and branch probabilities are not the spectral q_spec. Coherent phi(m^2), phi(beta^2) and phi((m+/-beta)^2) terms remain in the source ledger and its validation.

## Actual growth and the unavoidable controller frontier

With one exclusive selector of probability1/4, constant fallback, core u=(1,1,1,-1) and C=4, the core reserve is zero. R_root=3/8 and the nonconstant first-data child has R=1/2, a strict1/8 increase. The other child has R=0. This is a physical growing branch with controlled conditional expectation and eventual zero data-prefix reserve.

For any arbitrary Boolean controller seed h, take two independent dictator cores and F=((1+h)/2)y0+((1-h)/2)y1. Query the two data bits first. When y0=y1 the controller leaf is constant; otherwise it is+/-h. Thus exactly

    E H(F_sigma)=H(h)/2,
    E I(F_sigma)=I(h)/2=I_ctrl(F),
    E Q_alpha(F_sigma,declared T)=Q_alpha(h,T)/2

for consistently sign-translated copies of a stated control tree. Both core reserves are zero. A universal linear entropy payment of this frontier is full FEI. Its reserve cannot be removed because the data prefix and its outer bank were paid. This is a source-specific boundary obstruction for the new lift, not a claim that the controller class was previously known to be easy.

First unsupported universal line: a physical policy supplies dimension-independent initial bounds for the inherited arbitrary-core reserves and its ACTUAL controller-boundary debit from unused exclusive influence. The new theorem transports such certificates and pays the positive outer-prefix excess; it does not prove their universal existence. Controls inside the data prefix or overlapping physical data between selected cores require new terms and are not covered.

# A066 / B14 — semigroup dissipation

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every finite-dimensional Boolean sign f on the uniform cube, write p_S=hat(f)(S)^2, I(f)=sum |S|p_S, and H_nat(f)=sum p_S ln(1/p_S). Entropy in bits is H_nat/ln(2). The objective of this packet is an exact noise-semigroup integral identity, its boundary terms and endpoint integrability, and the precise unproved universal influence payment.

**Outcome:** this packet's identity and endpoint deliverables are complete locally. A sufficient integrable pointwise influence-envelope mechanism is analytically ruled out by actual Boolean point defects. The universal integrated influence bound and full FEI remain OPEN. Both authored claims await independent review; no novelty or priority claim is made.

The semigroup is T_t=exp(-tL), L chi_S=|S|chi_S. Its squared spectrum q_t(S)=p_S exp(-2t|S|) is unnormalized. Define F(t)=sum q_t ln(1/q_t), E(t)=sum |S|q_t and G_f(t)=F(t)-2tE(t). This corrected entropy tracks the initial spectral information; it is not the entropy of a renormalized evolving law. Set

J_f(t)=sum |S|p_S exp(-2t|S|)ln(1/p_S).

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete finite-sum proof is in `DERIVATION.md`. Differentiation yields G_f'=-2J_f and the exact identity

H_nat(f)=p_empty ln(1/p_empty)+2 integral_0^infinity J_f(t) dt.

The boundary satisfies 0<=p_empty ln(1/p_empty)<=I(f). For each fixed f, J_f(t)<=J_f(0) near zero and J_f(t)<=exp(-2t)J_f(0) at infinity. The tail integral is at most J_f(0)exp(-2T)/2. Boolean coefficient granularity also gives J_f(0)<=2(n-1)ln(2)I(f) for n>=1; this bound retains dimension dependence.

**First unsupported line for full FEI:** there exists a finite universal K such that integral_0^infinity J_f(t)dt<=K I(f) for all finite-dimensional Boolean f. If proved, it would give the bit-entropy constant (1+2K)/ln(2). Conversely FEI implies that integral bound with K=C ln(2)/2. The integral identity is therefore an explicit equivalent open target, not a universal payment.

For the actual Boolean family f_n=1-2 1_{x=(1,...,1)}, n>=2,

J_{f_n}(t)/I(f_n)=(2n-2)ln(2) exp(-2t)[(1+exp(-2t))/2]^(n-1).

Choosing n=ceil(1/t) proves that any dimension-independent measurable pointwise envelope k(t) must satisfy k(t)>=ln(2)exp(-3/2)/t almost everywhere on (0,1/4]. Such an envelope cannot be integrable. However the exact area is

(integral J_{f_n})/I(f_n)=2(1-1/n)(1-2^(-n))ln(2)<=2ln(2).

This rules out the pointwise-envelope mechanism while leaving the integrated target intact. It already fails on a simple threshold family. Full signed Booleanity is not used by the identity; a future universal payment must use additional Boolean structure. The normalized, non-Boolean singleton law has I=1 and integral J=(ln n)/2, so normalization alone is insufficient.
